	local function fn23()
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return Players.LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing()
		end)

		if ok and type(result) == "number" and result == result then
			return math.clamp(result, 0, 0.5)
		end
		return 0.075
	end

	local function getRevolverPredictionTime()
		local now = os.clock()
		local v29 = fn23()
		local n3 = math.clamp(now - (mellstroySilentAimState.LastPingSampleTime or now), 0.0041666666666666666, 0.5)
		local smoothedNetworkPing = mellstroySilentAimState.SmoothedNetworkPing
		local networkPingJitter = mellstroySilentAimState.NetworkPingJitter or 0
		local smoothedNetworkPing2

		if not mellstroySilentAimState.IsFiniteNumber(smoothedNetworkPing) then
			smoothedNetworkPing2 = v29
		else
			local n4 = 1 - math.exp(-6 * n3)
			local n5

			if not (smoothedNetworkPing < v29) then
				n5 = n4
			else
				n5 = math.max(n4, 0.35)
			end

			smoothedNetworkPing2 = smoothedNetworkPing + (v29 - smoothedNetworkPing) * n5
			networkPingJitter += (math.abs(v29 - smoothedNetworkPing) - networkPingJitter) * (1 - math.exp(-4 * n3))
		end

		mellstroySilentAimState.LastPingSampleTime = now
		mellstroySilentAimState.RawNetworkPing = v29
		mellstroySilentAimState.SmoothedNetworkPing = smoothedNetworkPing2
		mellstroySilentAimState.NetworkPingJitter = networkPingJitter
		mellstroySilentAimState.NetworkPingMs = smoothedNetworkPing2 * 1000
		mellstroySilentAimState.NetworkPingJitterMs = networkPingJitter * 1000
		local revolverPredictionTime = math.clamp(smoothedNetworkPing2 * 0.5 + math.min(networkPingJitter * 0.35, 0.025) + 0.016666666666666666, 0.025, 0.3)
		mellstroySilentAimState.RevolverPingFactor = 0.5
		mellstroySilentAimState.RevolverPredictionTime = revolverPredictionTime
		return revolverPredictionTime
	end

	local function getRevolverLead(arg, arg2, arg3)
		if not mellstroySilentAimState.IsFiniteVector3(arg) then
			return Vector3.zero
		end
		local n3 = math.clamp(tonumber(arg2) or 0, 0, 0.3)
		local n4 = arg * n3
		arg3 = arg3 and arg3:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

		if arg3 and arg3.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air then
			n4 += Vector3.new(0, -0.5 * workspace.Gravity * n3 * n3, 0)
		end

		local n5 = math.clamp(4 + n3 * 48, 4, 18)

		if n5 < n4.Magnitude then
			n4 = n4.Unit * n5
		end

		return mellstroySilentAimState.IsFiniteVector3(n4) and n4 or Vector3.zero
	end

	mellstroySilentAimState.GetRevolverPredictionTime = getRevolverPredictionTime
	mellstroySilentAimState.GetRevolverLead = getRevolverLead

	local function fn24(arg)
		local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not currentCamera then
			return nil
		end
		local mouseLocation = UserInputService2:GetMouseLocation()
		local huge = mellstroySilentAimState.RagebotFOV == math.huge and math.huge

		if not huge then
			huge = math.max(tonumber(mellstroySilentAimState.RagebotFOV) or 0, 0)
		end

		local v29 = nil
		local v30 = nil

		for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if not (player == Players.LocalPlayer or fn12(player).Whitelist or not fn16(player, arg)) then
				local character = player.Character
				local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local v31 = fn18(character)

				if not (not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not v31) then
					local v32 = fn21(player, v31)

					if v32 then
						local v33, v34 = currentCamera:WorldToScreenPoint(v32)

						if v34 and v33.Z > 0 then
							local magnitude = (Vector2.new(v33.X, v33.Y) - mouseLocation).Magnitude
							local n3 = math.max(tonumber(mellstroySilentAimState.HitboxExpansion[arg]) or 0, 0)
							local n4 = math.max(v31.Size.X, v31.Size.Y, v31.Size.Z) * 0.5 + n3

							if n4 > 0 then
								local v35 = currentCamera:WorldToScreenPoint(v32 + currentCamera.CFrame.RightVector * n4)

								if v35.Z > 0 then
									magnitude = math.max(0, magnitude - (Vector2.new(v35.X, v35.Y) - Vector2.new(v33.X, v33.Y)).Magnitude)
								end
							end

							if magnitude < huge then
								huge = magnitude
								v29 = player
								v30 = v32
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return v29, v30
	end

	local function fn25(arg)
		local v29 = arg and fn18(arg.Character)
		if not v29 then
			return nil
		end
		local v30 = mellstroySilentAimState.TargetSamples[arg]
		local v31 = fn21(arg, v29)
		local assemblyLinearVelocity = v29.AssemblyLinearVelocity
		assemblyLinearVelocity = mellstroySilentAimState.IsFiniteVector3(assemblyLinearVelocity) and assemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero

		if assemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 80 then
			assemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		end

		return v29, assemblyLinearVelocity, v30 and v30.Acceleration or Vector3.zero, v31
	end

	local function fn26(arg)
		local v29, v30, v31, v32 = fn25(arg)
		if not v29 or not v32 then
			return nil
		end
		local character = arg.Character
		local upperTorso = character and (character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
		if not upperTorso or not upperTorso:IsA("BasePart") then
			return nil
		end
		local n3 = v32 + upperTorso.Position - v29.Position
		local v33 = getRevolverPredictionTime()
		local n4 = n3 + getRevolverLead(v30, v33, character)
		if not mellstroySilentAimState.IsFiniteVector3(n4) then
			return nil
		end
		return n4, v33, v30, upperTorso, n3
	end

	local function fn27(arg, arg2)
		if typeof(arg) ~= "Instance" then
			return false
		end
		local parent

		if arg2 == "Knife" then
			parent = arg.Parent and arg.Parent.Parent
		else
			parent = arg.Parent
		end

		if not parent or parent.Name ~= arg2 then
			return false
		end
		local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
		local backpack = localPlayer and localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
		return parent.Parent == localPlayer.Character or parent.Parent == backpack
	end

	local function fn28(arg)
		if arg.Name == "KnifeThrown" and fn27(arg, "Knife") then
			return "Knife"
		end

		if arg.Name == "Shoot" and fn27(arg, "Gun") then
			return "Revolver"
		end
		return nil
	end

	mellstroySilentAimState.Rewrite = function(arg, arg2)
		local v29 = fn28(arg)
		if not v29 then
			return false
		end

		if not (mellstroySilentAimState.Enabled and mellstroySilentAimState.WeaponSettings[v29]) then
			if v29 ~= "Revolver" or not mellstroySilentAimState.ForceNoSpread or arg2.n ~= 2 or not mellstroySilentAimState.IsFiniteCFrame(arg2[1]) then
				return false
			end
			local mouse = Players.LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
			mouse = mouse and mouse.Hit
			if not mellstroySilentAimState.IsFiniteCFrame(mouse) then
				return false
			end
			arg2[2] = CFrame.new(mouse.Position)
			return true, false
		end

		local flag2 = v29 == "Knife"
		mellstroySilentAimState.EquippedWeapon = v29
		local v30, position = fn24(v29)
		if not v30 then
			return false
		end
		local v31 = arg2[1]
		if arg2.n ~= 2 or not mellstroySilentAimState.IsFiniteCFrame(v31) or not mellstroySilentAimState.IsFiniteCFrame(arg2[2]) then
			return false
		end
		local v32 = nil
		local position2 = v31.Position
		local cframe, flag3, flag4, n3, v33, v34, flag5, v35, v36

		if flag2 then
			if mellstroySilentAimState.MagicKnife then
				local v37
				v37, v32 = mellstroySilentAimState.GetClosestCharacterPoint(v30.Character, v31.Position)
				if not v37 or not v32 then
					return false
				end
				position = v32.Position
				local parent = arg.Parent
				local knifeStabbed = parent and parent:FindFirstChild("KnifeStabbed")
				local handleTouched = parent and parent:FindFirstChild("HandleTouched")
				if not knifeStabbed or not knifeStabbed:IsA("RemoteEvent") or not handleTouched or not handleTouched:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
					return false
				end
				cframe = CFrame.new(position)
				knifeStabbed:FireServer()

				task.delay(0.03, function()
					if v32.Parent and handleTouched.Parent then
						handleTouched:FireServer(v32)
					end
				end)

				flag3 = true
				flag4 = true
				n3 = 0
				v33 = position
			else
				if not mellstroySilentAimState.IsFiniteVector3(position) then
					return false
				end
				n3 = math.clamp((position - v31.Position).Magnitude / n2, 0, 2.5)
				flag3 = false
				flag4 = false
				cframe = nil
				v33 = nil
			end

			v34 = nil
			flag5 = false
			v35 = nil
			v36 = v31
		else
			position, n3, v34, v32, v35 = fn26(v30)
			local wallbang = position and mellstroySilentAimState.Wallbang

			if wallbang then
				wallbang = not mellstroySilentAimState.IsPointVisible(v30.Character, v31.Position, v35 or position)
			end

			flag5 = false
			flag3 = false
			flag4 = false
			cframe = nil
			v33 = nil

			if wallbang then
				v36, cframe, position2, v33 = mellstroySilentAimState.BuildRevolverWallbang(v31, position)
				if not v36 or not cframe then
					return false
				end
				flag5 = true
				flag3 = true
			else
				v36 = v31
			end
		end

		if not mellstroySilentAimState.IsFiniteVector3(position) then
			return false
		end
		cframe = cframe or CFrame.new(position)
		v33 = v33 or position
		arg2[1] = v36
		arg2[2] = cframe
		local humanoid = v30.Character and v30.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

		mellstroySilentAimState.LastRewrite = {
			Time = os.clock(),
			Weapon = v29,
			Target = v30.Name,
			OriginPosition = v31.Position,
			RemoteOriginPosition = position2,
			AimPosition = position,
			BaseAimPosition = v35,
			RemoteEndpointPosition = v33,
			Hitbox = v32 and v32.Name or nil,
			TargetHealth = humanoid and humanoid.Health or nil,
			FlightTime = n3,
			TargetVelocity = v34,
			ThroughWall = flag3,
		}

		mellstroySilentAimState.LastRewrite.Kind = "manual"
		mellstroySilentAimState.RecordShot(mellstroySilentAimState.LastRewrite)
		if flag5 then
			mellstroySilentAimState.QueueRevolverWallbang(arg, v30.Character, position, v32, v35, n3, mellstroySilentAimState.LastRewrite)
			return true, true
		end
		return true, flag4
	end

	mellstroySilentAimState.TagFeedbackCall = function(arg, arg2)
		local mellstroyFeedback = getgenv().__MellstroyFeedback
		if not mellstroyFeedback or type(mellstroyFeedback.Tag) ~= "function" then
			return
		end

		if typeof(arg) == "Instance" and arg.Name == "HandleTouched" and fn27(arg, "Knife") then
			arg2 = arg2 and arg2[1]
			local model = typeof(arg2) == "Instance" and arg2:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
			local playerFromCharacter = model and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model)

			if playerFromCharacter then
				mellstroyFeedback:Tag(playerFromCharacter, "Knife")
			end

			return
		end

		local v29 = fn28(arg)
		arg2 = arg2 and arg2[2]
		if not v29 or not mellstroySilentAimState.IsFiniteCFrame(arg2) then
			return
		end
		local position = arg2.Position
		local n3 = 10
		local v30 = nil

		for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if player ~= Players.LocalPlayer then
				local character = player.Character
				local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
					local magnitude = mellstroySilentAimState.GetClosestCharacterPoint(character, position)
					magnitude = magnitude and (magnitude - position).Magnitude

					if magnitude and magnitude < n3 then
						n3 = magnitude
						v30 = player
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if v30 then
			mellstroyFeedback:Tag(v30, v29)
		end
	end

	if not mellstroySilentAimState.HookInstalled then
		local v29 = nil

		local function fn29(arg, ...)
			local v30 = table.pack(...)
			local v31 = getnamecallmethod()
			local flag2 = type(checkcaller) == "function" and checkcaller()
			local flag3 = (mellstroySilentAimState.Enabled or mellstroySilentAimState.ForceNoSpread) and not flag2 and v31 == "FireServer"
			local flag4 = false

			if flag3 then
				local rewrite = mellstroySilentAimState.Rewrite

				if type(rewrite) == "function" then
					local ok, result, result2 = pcall(rewrite, arg, v30)
					flag4 = ok and result2 == true
				end
			end

			if v31 == "FireServer" then
				local tagFeedbackCall = mellstroySilentAimState.TagFeedbackCall

				if type(tagFeedbackCall) == "function" then
					pcall(tagFeedbackCall, arg, v30)
				end
			end

			if flag4 then
				return nil
			end

			if type(setnamecallmethod) == "function" then
				setnamecallmethod(v31)
			end

			return v29(arg, table.unpack(v30, 1, v30.n))
		end

		v29 = hookmetamethod
		v29 = v29(game, "__namecall", newcclosure(fn29))
		mellstroySilentAimState.OldNamecall = v29
		mellstroySilentAimState.HookInstalled = true
	end

				Position = UDim2.new(1, -68, 0, 3),
				Size = UDim2.fromOffset(56, 28),
				ZIndex = 17,
				Image = tbl11[v30],
				ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(135, 135, 135),
				ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
			})

			local TextButton2 = tbl:RenderObject("TextButton", {
				Name = "SilentAimWeaponOption" .. v30,
				Active = true,
				AutoButtonColor = false,
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				BorderSizePixel = 0,
				Interactable = true,
				Parent = Frame6,
				Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
				Text = "",
				ZIndex = 19,
			})

			tbl13[i] = { Frame = Frame6, Square = Frame8, Button = TextButton2 }

			tbl:CreateConnection(TextButton2.MouseEnter, function()
				Frame6.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(42, 42, 42)
			end)

			tbl:CreateConnection(TextButton2.MouseLeave, function()
				Frame6.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
			end)

			tbl:CreateConnection(TextButton2.Activated, function()
				fn31("Option" .. i, function()
					tbl12:Set(i)
					tbl12:SetOpen(false)
				end)
			end)
		end

		tbl12.Set = function(arg4, state)
			if not arg2[state] then
				return
			end
			tbl12.State = state
			local v30 = arg2[state]
			TextLabel.Text = v30
			ImageLabel.Image = tbl11[v30]

			for i, v31 in ipairs(tbl13) do
				v31.Square.BackgroundColor3 = i == state and arg.Window.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(65, 65, 65)
			end

			arg3(state)
		end

		tbl12.Get = function()
			return tbl12.State
		end

		tbl12.SetOpen = function(arg4, open)
			tbl12.Open = open
			Frame4.Visible = open
			ImageLabel2.Rotation = open and 180 or 0
			Frame3.BackgroundColor3 = open and Color3.fromRGB(42, 42, 42) or Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
		end

		tbl12.Close = function()
			tbl12:SetOpen(false)
		end

		local function fn33(arg4, arg5)
			local absolutePosition = arg4.AbsolutePosition
			local absoluteSize = arg4.AbsoluteSize
			return arg5.X >= absolutePosition.X and arg5.Y >= absolutePosition.Y and arg5.X <= absolutePosition.X + absoluteSize.X and arg5.Y <= absolutePosition.Y + absoluteSize.Y
		end

		tbl:CreateConnection(TextButton.MouseEnter, function()
			Frame3.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(42, 42, 42)
		end)

		tbl:CreateConnection(TextButton.MouseLeave, function()
			if not tbl12.Open then
				Frame3.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
			end
		end)

		tbl:CreateConnection(TextButton.Activated, function()
			fn31("Main", function()
				tbl12:SetOpen(not tbl12.Open)
			end)
		end)

		tbl:CreateConnection(UserInputService2.InputBegan, function(arg4)
			if arg4.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and arg4.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				return
			end
			local now = os.clock()
			local v30 = tbl:MouseLocation(arg4)

			task.defer(function()
				if now - 0.1 <= n4 then
					return
				end

				if fn33(TextButton, v30) then
					fn30("Main", function()
						tbl12:SetOpen(not tbl12.Open)
					end)

					return
				end

				if not tbl12.Open then
					return
				end

				for i, v31 in ipairs(tbl13) do
					if fn33(v31.Button, v30) then
						fn30("Option" .. i, function()
							tbl12:Set(i)
							tbl12:SetOpen(false)
						end)

						return
					end
				end

				tbl12:SetOpen(false)
			end)
		end)

		tbl12:Set(1)
		return tbl12
	end

	local function fn32()
		local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not currentCamera then
			return nil
		end
		local mouseLocation = UserInputService2:GetMouseLocation()
		local vector2 = Vector2.new(mouseLocation.X, mouseLocation.Y)
		local v32 = currentCamera:ScreenPointToRay(vector2.X, vector2.Y)
		local origin = v32.Origin
		local unit = v32.Direction.Unit
		local n3 = math.max(tonumber(mellstroySilentAimState.HitboxExpansion.Revolver) or 0, 0)
		local flag4 = mellstroySilentAimState.RagebotFOV == math.huge
		local ragebotFOV = mellstroySilentAimState.RagebotFOV
		local position = mellstroySilentAimState.GetGunShotOrigin()
		position = position and position.Position or origin
		local v33 = nil
		local v34 = nil
		local v35 = nil

		for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if not (player == Players.LocalPlayer or fn12(player).Whitelist or not fn31(player)) then
				local character = player.Character
				local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if not (not character or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0) then
					local v36 = fn18(character)

					if v36 then
						for k, v37 in pairs(tbl13) do
							if mellstroySilentAimState.RagebotHitboxes[k] then
								for _, v38 in ipairs(v37) do
									local v39 = character:FindFirstChild(v38)

									if v39 and v39:IsA("BasePart") then
										local v40 = mellstroySilentAimState.GetHitboxExpansionPadding(v39, n3)
										local v41

										if flag4 then
											v41 = mellstroySilentAimState.GetClosestPointOnPart(v39, position, v40)
										else
											local n4 = math.max(0, (v39.Position - origin):Dot(unit))
											v41 = nil

											for i = 1, 5 do
												local v42 = mellstroySilentAimState.GetClosestPointOnPart(v39, origin + unit * n4, v40)

												if v42 then
													n4 = math.max(0, (v42 - origin):Dot(unit))
													v41 = v42
													continue
												end

												break
											end
										end

										if v41 then
											local v42 = mellstroySilentAimState.GetClosestPointOnPart(v39, v41)

											if v42 then
												local v43, v44 = currentCamera:WorldToScreenPoint(v41)

												if flag4 or v44 and v43.Z > 0 then
													local n4

													if flag4 then
														n4 = (v36.Position - position).Magnitude * 100 + (tbl14[k] or 10)
													else
														n4 = flag4
													end

													n4 = n4 or (Vector2.new(v43.X, v43.Y) - vector2).Magnitude
													local v45 = v42:Lerp(v39.Position, 0.08)

													if (mellstroySilentAimState.Wallbang or mellstroySilentAimState.AutoFarmCombat or not mellstroySilentAimState.RagebotVisibleCheck or mellstroySilentAimState.IsPointVisible(character, position, v45)) and mellstroySilentAimState.IsFiniteNumber(n4) and mellstroySilentAimState.IsFiniteVector3(v45) and n4 < ragebotFOV then
														ragebotFOV = n4
														v33 = v39
														v34 = player
														v35 = v45
													end
												end
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return v33, v34, v35
	end

	tbl:CreateConnection(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
		if not mellstroySilentAimState.Ragebot and not mellstroySilentAimState.AutoFarmCombat then
			return
		end
		local now = os.clock()
		if now - (mellstroySilentAimState.LastRagebotFire or 0) < (mellstroySilentAimState.RagebotFireInterval or 3.08) then
			return
		end
		local v32, v33, v34 = fn32()
		if not v32 or not v33 or not mellstroySilentAimState.IsFiniteVector3(v34) then
			return
		end
		local position = v32.Position
		local v35, v36 = fn25(v33)
		local v37 = getRevolverPredictionTime()
		local finalAimPosition

		if mellstroySilentAimState.IsFiniteVector3(v36) then
			finalAimPosition = position + getRevolverLead(v36, v37, v33.Character)
		else
			finalAimPosition = position
		end

		if not mellstroySilentAimState.IsFiniteVector3(finalAimPosition) then
			return
		end
		local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
		local character = localPlayer.Character
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		character = character and character:FindFirstChild("Gun")

		if not character and humanoid then
			mellstroySilentAimState.LastRagebotGun = nil
			mellstroySilentAimState.RagebotGunReadyAt = nil
			local backpack = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
			backpack = backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Gun")

			if backpack and backpack:IsA("Tool") then
				humanoid:EquipTool(backpack)
			end

			return
		end

		if character ~= mellstroySilentAimState.LastRagebotGun then
			mellstroySilentAimState.LastRagebotGun = character
			mellstroySilentAimState.RagebotGunReadyAt = now + (mellstroySilentAimState.RagebotEquipDelay or 0.18)
			return
		end

		if now < (mellstroySilentAimState.RagebotGunReadyAt or 0) then
			return
		end
		character = character and character:FindFirstChild("Shoot")
		local v38 = mellstroySilentAimState.GetGunShotOrigin(finalAimPosition)
		if not v38 or not character or not character:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
			return
		end
		local wallbang = mellstroySilentAimState.Wallbang or mellstroySilentAimState.AutoFarmCombat
		local v39 = mellstroySilentAimState.IsPointVisible(v33.Character, v38.Position, position)
		if not wallbang and mellstroySilentAimState.RagebotVisibleCheck and not v39 then
			return
		end
		local cframe = CFrame.new(finalAimPosition)
		local position2 = v38.Position
		wallbang = wallbang and not v39
		local flag4 = false
		local v40, v41

		if wallbang then
			v40, cframe, position2, v41 = mellstroySilentAimState.BuildRevolverWallbang(v38, finalAimPosition)
			if not v40 or not cframe then
				return
			end
			flag4 = true
		else
			v40 = v38
			v41 = finalAimPosition
		end

		mellstroySilentAimState.LastRagebotFire = now
		local humanoid2 = v33.Character and v33.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

		mellstroySilentAimState.LastRagebotShot = {
			Time = now,
			Target = v33.Name,
			Hitbox = v32.Name,
			RawAimPosition = position,
			OriginPosition = v38.Position,
			RemoteOriginPosition = position2,
			AimPosition = finalAimPosition,
			RemoteEndpointPosition = v41,
			TargetHealth = humanoid2 and humanoid2.Health or nil,
			TargetVelocity = v36,
			PredictionTime = v37,
			PingMs = mellstroySilentAimState.NetworkPingMs,
			PingJitterMs = mellstroySilentAimState.NetworkPingJitterMs,
			ThroughWall = flag4,
		}

		mellstroySilentAimState.LastRagebotShot.Kind = "rage"
		mellstroySilentAimState.RecordShot(mellstroySilentAimState.LastRagebotShot)

		if flag4 then
			mellstroySilentAimState.QueueRevolverWallbang(character, v33.Character, finalAimPosition, v32, position, v37, mellstroySilentAimState.LastRagebotShot)
		else
			mellstroySilentAimState.LastRagebotShot.ActualFireTime = os.clock()
			mellstroySilentAimState.LastRagebotShot.FinalOriginPosition = position2
			mellstroySilentAimState.LastRagebotShot.FinalAimPosition = finalAimPosition
			character:FireServer(v40, cframe)

			task.delay(0.5, function()
				local lastRagebotShot = mellstroySilentAimState.LastRagebotShot
				if not lastRagebotShot or lastRagebotShot.Target ~= v33.Name then
					return
				end
				local humanoid3 = v33.Character and v33.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				lastRagebotShot.HealthAfter = humanoid3 and humanoid3.Health or 0
				lastRagebotShot.TargetPartAfter = v32.Parent and v32.Position or nil
			end)
		end
	end)

	tbl:CreateConnection(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
		if not mellstroySilentAimState.KnifeAura and not mellstroySilentAimState.AutoFarmCombat then
			return
		end
		local now = os.clock()
		if now - (mellstroySilentAimState.LastKnifeAuraStab or 0) < 0.9 then
			return
		end
		local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
		local character = localPlayer.Character
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local v32 = fn18(character)
		if not character or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not v32 then
			return
		end
		local knife = character:FindFirstChild("Knife")

		if not knife then
			local backpack = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
			backpack = backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Knife")

			if backpack and backpack:IsA("Tool") then
				humanoid:EquipTool(backpack)
			end

			return
		end

		local events = knife:FindFirstChild("Events")
		local knifeStabbed = events and events:FindFirstChild("KnifeStabbed")
		local handleTouched = events and events:FindFirstChild("HandleTouched")
		if not knifeStabbed or not knifeStabbed:IsA("RemoteEvent") or not handleTouched or not handleTouched:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
			return
		end
		local tbl15 = {}
		local n3 = math.max(tonumber(mellstroySilentAimState.HitboxExpansion.Knife) or 0, 0)

		for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if not (player == localPlayer or fn12(player).Whitelist or not fn16(player, "Knife")) then
				local character2 = player.Character
				local humanoid2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local v33 = fn18(character2)

				if humanoid2 and humanoid2.Health > 0 and v33 and (mellstroySilentAimState.AutoFarmCombat or (v33.Position - v32.Position).Magnitude <= mellstroySilentAimState.KnifeAuraRange + n3) then
					tbl15[#tbl15 + 1] = v33
				end
			end
		end

		if #tbl15 == 0 then
			return
		end
		mellstroySilentAimState.LastKnifeAuraStab = now
		knifeStabbed:FireServer()

		task.delay(0.03, function()
			if not mellstroySilentAimState.KnifeAura and not mellstroySilentAimState.AutoFarmCombat or not handleTouched.Parent then
				return
			end

			for _, v33 in ipairs(tbl15) do
				if v33.Parent then
					local mellstroyFeedback = getgenv().__MellstroyFeedback
					local playerFromCharacter = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(v33.Parent)

					if mellstroyFeedback and playerFromCharacter and type(mellstroyFeedback.Tag) == "function" then
						mellstroyFeedback:Tag(playerFromCharacter, "Knife")
					end

					handleTouched:FireServer(v33)
				end
			end
		end)
	end)

	local function fn33()
		local TweenService2 = game:GetService("TweenService")
		local MarketplaceService = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")
		local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
		local n3 = 60
		local flag4 = false

		local function fn34(arg)
			local flag5 = arg == true
			if flag5 == flag4 then
				return
			end
			flag4 = flag5
			if type(setfpscap) ~= "function" then
				return
			end

			if flag5 then
				if type(getfpscap) == "function" then
					local ok, result = pcall(getfpscap)

					if ok and type(result) == "number" and result > 0 then
						n3 = result
					end
				end

				pcall(setfpscap, 5)
			else
				pcall(setfpscap, n3)
			end
		end

		local function fn35()
			local v32 = getcustomasset or getsynasset
			local str = index.Folder .. "/fonts/Verdana.ttf"
			local str2 = index.Folder .. "/fonts/Verdana.font.json"
			if type(isfile) ~= "function" or type(writefile) ~= "function" or type(v32) ~= "function" then
				return Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.Arial)
			end

			if not isfile(str) then
				return Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.Arial)
			end
			local ok, result = pcall(v32, str)
			if not ok then
				return Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.Arial)
			end
			local json = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode({ name = "Verdana", faces = { { name = "Regular", weight = 400, style = "normal", assetId = result } } })
			if not pcall(writefile, str2, json) then
				return Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.Arial)
			end
			local ok2, result2 = pcall(v32, str2)
			if not ok2 then
				return Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.Arial)
			end
			local ok3, result3 = pcall(Font.new, result2, Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
			return ok3 and result3 or Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.Arial)
		end

		local v32 = fn35()

		local tbl15 = {
			Enabled = false,
			Aggressive = false,
			NextUpdate = 0,
			OptimizedModels = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
			ProfileData = nil,
		}

		local ok, profileData = pcall(function()
			return require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.ProfileData)
		end)

			if not arg2 or not arg2.Parent then
				return false
			end
			arg.OptimizedModels[arg2] = true
			local descendants = arg2:GetDescendants()
			local flag5 = false

			for i = #descendants, 1, -1 do
				local v33 = descendants[i]

				if not (arg3 and (v33 == arg3 or v33:IsDescendantOf(arg3))) and (v33:IsA("BasePart") or v33:IsA("Decal") or v33:IsA("Texture") or v33:IsA("DataModelMesh") or v33:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") or v33:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v33:IsA("Trail") or v33:IsA("Beam") or v33:IsA("Sound")) then
					flag5 = pcall(v33.Destroy, v33) or flag5
				end
			end

			return flag5
		end

				return

					flag5 = arg:StripModel(child, nil) or flag5
				elseif coinContainer then
					flag5 = arg:StripModel(child, coinContainer) or flag5
				end
			end

				pcall(collectgarbage, "collect")
			end
		end

			if not arg.Enabled then
				return
			end
			arg.Status.Text = arg3 and arg2 or "[ disabled ] autofarm is off"
			local now = os.clock()
			if now < arg.NextUpdate then
				return
			end
			arg.NextUpdate = now + 0.5
			arg:PurgeGui()
			arg.Balance.Text = "current balance: " .. (arg:GetBalance() or "?")
			arg:OptimizeVisibleWorld()
		end

			if not arg.Aggressive then
				return
			end

			if playerGui then
				for _, child in ipairs(playerGui:GetChildren()) do
					pcall(child.Destroy, child)
				end
			end

			local v33 = ipairs
			local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")

			for _, child in v33(StarterGui:GetChildren()) do
				pcall(child.Destroy, child)
			end

			for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				local character = player.Character

				if character then
					for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("Accessory") or child:IsA("Clothing") or child:IsA("ShirtGraphic") or child:IsA("CharacterMesh") then
							pcall(child.Destroy, child)
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if not arg.StaticAssetsPurged then
				arg.StaticAssetsPurged = true
				local descendants = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):GetDescendants()

				for i = #descendants, 1, -1 do
					local v34 = descendants[i]

					if v34:IsA("GuiObject") or v34:IsA("LayerCollector") or v34:IsA("BasePart") or v34:IsA("Decal") or v34:IsA("Texture") or v34:IsA("DataModelMesh") or v34:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") or v34:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v34:IsA("Trail") or v34:IsA("Beam") or v34:IsA("Sound") or v34:IsA("Animation") then
						pcall(v34.Destroy, v34)
					end
				end
			end

			for _, child in ipairs(v.ScreenGui:GetChildren()) do
				if child ~= arg.Overlay and child ~= arg.PreservedStatus then
					pcall(child.Destroy, child)
				end
			end
		end

		end

			Enabled = false,
			Generation = 0,
			CurrentMap = nil,
			CurrentTween = nil,

			Elite = false,
			BagLimit = 40,

			DisplayCount = 0,
			HasGuiCount = false,
			WaitingLobby = false,
			ResetRequested = false,
			NeedsFirstCoinTeleport = false,

		}

		getgenv().__MellstroyCoinFarmState = mellstroyCoinFarmState

		local function fn36()
			local currentTween = mellstroyCoinFarmState.CurrentTween
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.CurrentTween = nil

			if currentTween then
				pcall(function()
					currentTween:Cancel()
				end)
			end
		end

		local function fn37(arg)
			local combatActive = arg == true

			if mellstroyCoinFarmState.CombatActive ~= combatActive then
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.CombatActive = combatActive
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.LastCombatTransition = os.clock()
			end

			mellstroySilentAimState.AutoFarmCombat = combatActive
			local character = localPlayer.Character
			local backpack = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
			character = character and character:FindFirstChild("Gun") ~= nil or backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Gun") ~= nil
			tbl4:SetAutomatic(combatActive and not character)
		end

		local function fn38(arg)
			local character = localPlayer.Character
			local backpack = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
			return character and character:FindFirstChild(arg) ~= nil or backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild(arg) ~= nil
		end

		local function fn39()
			local lobbyRoot = mellstroyCoinFarmState.LobbyRoot

			if lobbyRoot and lobbyRoot.Parent then
				pcall(function()
					lobbyRoot.Anchored = mellstroyCoinFarmState.LobbyAnchored == true
				end)
			end

			mellstroyCoinFarmState.LobbyRoot = nil
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.LobbyCFrame = nil
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.LobbyAnchored = false
		end

		local function fn40()
			fn36()
			fn37(false)
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.WaitingLobby = true
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.NeedsFirstCoinTeleport = false
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.RoundRoot = nil
		end

		local function fn41()
			if not mellstroyCoinFarmState.Enabled or not mellstroyCoinFarmState.WaitingLobby then
				return
			end
			local character = localPlayer.Character
			local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not character then
				return
			end

			if mellstroyCoinFarmState.LobbyRoot ~= character then
				fn39()
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.LobbyRoot = character
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.LobbyAnchored = character.Anchored
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.LobbyCFrame = character.CFrame - Vector3.new(0, 12, 0)
			end

			character.Anchored = true
			character.CFrame = mellstroyCoinFarmState.LobbyCFrame
			character.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			character.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			humanoid:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
		end

		local function fn42()
			textLabel.Visible = mellstroyCoinFarmState.Enabled and not tbl15.Enabled
			if not mellstroyCoinFarmState.Enabled then
				return
			end

			if mellstroyCoinFarmState.WaitingLobby or not mellstroyCoinFarmState.CurrentMap then
				textLabel.Text = "[ lobby ] waiting next round"
			elseif not mellstroyCoinFarmState.FinishedRound then
				local bagLimit = mellstroyCoinFarmState.BagLimit
				local bagLimit2 = mellstroyCoinFarmState.BagLimit
				textLabel.Text = string.format("[ %d / %d ] farming coins", math.clamp(math.floor(mellstroyCoinFarmState.DisplayCount), 0, bagLimit), bagLimit2)
			elseif fn38("Knife") or fn14(fn8(localPlayer)) then
				textLabel.Text = "[ ragebot ] killing everyone"
			else
				textLabel.Text = "[ ragebot ] killing murderer"
			end
		end

		local function fn43()
			for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
				if child:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer", true) then
					return child
				end
			end

			return nil
		end

		local function fn44(arg)
			if arg:IsA("BasePart") then
				return arg.CFrame
			end

			if arg:IsA("Model") then
				local ok2, result = pcall(arg.GetPivot, arg)
				return ok2 and result or nil
			end
			local basePart = arg:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
			if basePart then
				return basePart.CFrame
			end
			return nil
		end

		local function fn45(arg)
			if not arg or not arg.Parent then
				return false
			end
			local coinVisual = arg:FindFirstChild("CoinVisual", true)
			if not coinVisual then
				return true
			end
			local mainCoin = coinVisual:FindFirstChild("MainCoin", true)
			if mainCoin and mainCoin:IsA("BasePart") then
				return mainCoin.Transparency < 0.95
			end

			for _, descendant in ipairs(coinVisual:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and descendant.Transparency < 0.95 then
					return true
				end
			end

			return not coinVisual:IsA("BasePart") or coinVisual.Transparency < 0.95
		end

		local function fn46(arg, arg2, arg3)
			arg = arg and arg:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer", true)
			if not arg then
				return nil
			end
			local huge = math.huge
			local v34 = nil
			local v35 = nil

			for _, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
				if not (descendant.Name ~= "Coin_Server" or mellstroyCoinFarmState.IgnoredCoins[descendant] or not arg3 and not fn45(descendant)) then
					local v36 = fn44(descendant)

					if v36 then
						local magnitude = (v36.Position - arg2.Position).Magnitude

						if magnitude < huge then
							huge = magnitude
							v34 = descendant
							v35 = v36
						end
					end
				end
			end

			return v34, v35, huge
		end

		local function fn47()
			local playerGui = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
			playerGui = playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("MainGUI")
			if not playerGui then
				return nil
			end

			for _, v34 in ipairs({ "Game", "Lobby" }) do
				local coinBags = playerGui:FindFirstChild(v34)
				coinBags = coinBags and coinBags:FindFirstChild("CoinBags")
				coinBags = coinBags and coinBags:FindFirstChild("Container")
				coinBags = coinBags and coinBags:FindFirstChild("Coin")
				coinBags = coinBags and coinBags:FindFirstChild("Coins", true)

				if coinBags and (coinBags:IsA("TextLabel") or coinBags:IsA("TextButton")) then
					local tbl16 = {}

					for match in tostring(coinBags.Text):gmatch("%d+") do
						tbl16[#tbl16 + 1] = tonumber(match)
					end

					if tbl16[1] then
						return tbl16[1], tbl16[2]
					end
				end
			end

			return nil
		end

		local function fn48(currentMap)
			fn36()
			fn39()
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.CurrentMap = currentMap
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.FinishedRound = false
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.FallbackCount = 0
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.DisplayCount = 0
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.HasGuiCount = false
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.WaitingLobby = false
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.ResetRequested = false
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.NeedsFirstCoinTeleport = true
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.RoundRoot = nil
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.IgnoredCoins = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
			fn37(false)
			fn42()
		end

		local function fn49()
			if mellstroyCoinFarmState.FinishedRound then
				return
			end
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.FinishedRound = true
			fn36()

			if fn8(localPlayer) == "Innocent" and not fn38("Gun") then
				fn40()

				if not mellstroyCoinFarmState.ResetRequested then
					mellstroyCoinFarmState.ResetRequested = true
					local character = localPlayer.Character
					character = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

					if character and character.Health > 0 then
						character.Health = 0
					end
				end

				fn42()
				return
			end

			fn37(true)
			fn42()
		end

		local function fn50()
			local v34, v35 = fn47()

			if type(v35) == "number" and v35 >= 40 then
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.BagLimit = v35
			end

			local displayCount

			if type(v34) == "number" then
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.HasGuiCount = true
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.DisplayCount = math.max(v34, mellstroyCoinFarmState.FallbackCount)
				displayCount = mellstroyCoinFarmState.DisplayCount
			elseif mellstroyCoinFarmState.HasGuiCount then
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.DisplayCount = math.max(mellstroyCoinFarmState.DisplayCount, mellstroyCoinFarmState.FallbackCount)
				displayCount = mellstroyCoinFarmState.DisplayCount
			else
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.DisplayCount = mellstroyCoinFarmState.FallbackCount
				displayCount = mellstroyCoinFarmState.FallbackCount
			end

			if displayCount >= mellstroyCoinFarmState.BagLimit then
				fn49()
				return true
			end
			return false
		end

		local function fn51(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5, arg6)
			local tween = TweenService2:Create(arg2, TweenInfo.new(math.max(arg5 / math.max(mellstroyCoinFarmState.TweenSpeed, 1), 0.05), Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { CFrame = arg4 })
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.CurrentTween = tween
			local flag5 = false
			local v34 = nil

			local connection = tween.Completed:Connect(function(playbackState)
				v34 = playbackState
				flag5 = true
			end)

			tween:Play()
			local flag6

			while true do
				local flag7 = not flag5 and mellstroyCoinFarmState.Enabled and mellstroyCoinFarmState.Generation == arg6
				flag6 = false

				if flag7 then
					if mellstroyCoinFarmState.CurrentMap ~= arg or not arg2.Parent or arg2.Parent ~= localPlayer.Character then
						tween:Cancel()
						break
					elseif not fn45(arg3) then
						flag6 = (arg2.Position - arg4.Position).Magnitude <= 8
						tween:Cancel()
						break
					else
						RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
						continue
					end
				end

				break
			end

			connection:Disconnect()

			if mellstroyCoinFarmState.CurrentTween == tween then
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.CurrentTween = nil
			end

			if (flag6 or v34 == Enum.PlaybackState.Completed) and mellstroyCoinFarmState.Enabled and mellstroyCoinFarmState.Generation == arg6 then
				task.wait(0.12)

				if not fn45(arg3) then
					mellstroyCoinFarmState.IgnoredCoins[arg3] = true
					mellstroyCoinFarmState.FallbackCount = mellstroyCoinFarmState.FallbackCount + 1

					if not mellstroyCoinFarmState.HasGuiCount then
						mellstroyCoinFarmState.DisplayCount = mellstroyCoinFarmState.FallbackCount
					end
				end

				task.wait(mellstroyCoinFarmState.TweenDelay)
			end
		end

		local function fn52(arg)
			task.spawn(function()
				local ok2, result = pcall(function()
					return MarketplaceService:UserOwnsGamePassAsync(localPlayer.UserId, 429957)
				end)

				if mellstroyCoinFarmState.Enabled and mellstroyCoinFarmState.Generation == arg and ok2 then
					mellstroyCoinFarmState.Elite = result == true
					mellstroyCoinFarmState.BagLimit = mellstroyCoinFarmState.Elite and 50 or 40
				end
			end)

			while mellstroyCoinFarmState.Enabled and mellstroyCoinFarmState.Generation == arg do
				local v34 = fn43()

				if v34 ~= mellstroyCoinFarmState.CurrentMap then
					if v34 then
						fn48(v34)
					else
						fn36()
						mellstroyCoinFarmState.CurrentMap = nil
						fn40()
					end
				end

				if not v34 and not mellstroyCoinFarmState.WaitingLobby then
					fn40()
				end

				if v34 and not mellstroyCoinFarmState.FinishedRound and not fn50() then
					local character = localPlayer.Character
					local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and character and character:IsA("BasePart") then
						if mellstroyCoinFarmState.RoundRoot ~= character then
							fn36()
							mellstroyCoinFarmState.RoundRoot = character
							mellstroyCoinFarmState.NeedsFirstCoinTeleport = true
						end

						local v35, v36, v37 = fn46(v34, character, mellstroyCoinFarmState.NeedsFirstCoinTeleport)

						if v35 and v36 then
							if mellstroyCoinFarmState.NeedsFirstCoinTeleport then
								mellstroyCoinFarmState.NeedsFirstCoinTeleport = false
								character.Anchored = false
								character.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
								character.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
								character.CFrame = v36
								task.wait(0.12)

								if not fn45(v35) then
									mellstroyCoinFarmState.IgnoredCoins[v35] = true
									mellstroyCoinFarmState.FallbackCount = mellstroyCoinFarmState.FallbackCount + 1
									mellstroyCoinFarmState.DisplayCount = math.max(mellstroyCoinFarmState.DisplayCount, mellstroyCoinFarmState.FallbackCount)
								end

								task.wait(mellstroyCoinFarmState.TweenDelay)
							else
								fn51(v34, character, v35, v36, v37, arg)
							end
						else
							task.wait(0.02)
						end
					else
						task.wait(0.15)
					end
				else
					task.wait(0.15)
				end
			end
		end

		local function fn53(arg)
			local enabled = arg == true
			if mellstroyCoinFarmState.Enabled == enabled then
				return
			end
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.Enabled = enabled
			mellstroyCoinFarmState.Generation = mellstroyCoinFarmState.Generation + 1
			fn36()

			if enabled then
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.CurrentMap = nil
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.FinishedRound = false
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.FallbackCount = 0
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.DisplayCount = 0
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.HasGuiCount = false
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.WaitingLobby = false
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.ResetRequested = false
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.NeedsFirstCoinTeleport = false
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.RoundRoot = nil
				fn37(false)
				fn42()
				task.spawn(fn52, mellstroyCoinFarmState.Generation)
			else
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.CurrentMap = nil
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.FinishedRound = false
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.WaitingLobby = false
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.ResetRequested = false
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.NeedsFirstCoinTeleport = false
				mellstroyCoinFarmState.RoundRoot = nil
				fn37(false)
				fn39()
				fn42()
			end
		end

		tbl:CreateConnection(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
			fn41()

	local function fn37(arg)
		if not arg then

	local function fn38(arg)
		local v33 = fn37(arg)
		if not v33 then
			return nil
		end
		local now = os.clock()

		return {
			Character = arg,
			Root = v33,
			OriginPosition = v33.Position,
			LastPosition = v33.Position,
			LastSampleTime = now,
			StartTime = now,
			BaselineAssemblySpeed = v33.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude,
			CandidateStartTime = nil,
			CandidateSamples = 0,
			CandidateReason = nil,
		}
	end

	local function fn39(arg, arg2, arg3)
		if not arg2 or not arg2.Parent then
			return true, "target character removed"
		end

		if not arg3 or not arg3.Parent or arg3 ~= arg.Root then
			return true, "target root changed"
		end
		local humanoid = arg2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if humanoid and humanoid.Health <= 0 then
			return true, "target died"
		end
		local now = os.clock()
		local position = arg3.Position
		local n16 = (position - arg.LastPosition).Magnitude / math.max(now - arg.LastSampleTime, 0.0041666666666666666)
		local magnitude = arg3.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude
		local magnitude2 = (position - arg.OriginPosition).Magnitude
		arg.LastPosition = position
		arg.LastSampleTime = now
		if now - arg.StartTime < n8 then
			return false
		end
		local candidateReason

		if math.max(120, arg.BaselineAssemblySpeed + n14) <= magnitude then
			candidateReason = "target velocity"
		elseif n16 >= math.max(140, arg.BaselineAssemblySpeed + n15) then
			candidateReason = "target displacement speed"
		else
			local flag7 = magnitude2 >= n12 and n16 >= math.max(50, arg.BaselineAssemblySpeed + n13)
			candidateReason = nil

			if flag7 then
				candidateReason = "target displacement"
			end
		end

		if not arg.CandidateStartTime then
			if candidateReason then
				arg.CandidateStartTime = now
				arg.CandidateSamples = 1
				arg.CandidateReason = candidateReason
			end

			return false
		end

		if not candidateReason and magnitude2 < n11 then
			arg.CandidateStartTime = nil
			arg.CandidateSamples = 0
			arg.CandidateReason = nil
			return false
		end

		arg.CandidateSamples = arg.CandidateSamples + 1
		if now - arg.CandidateStartTime >= n9 and arg.CandidateSamples >= n10 then
			return true, arg.CandidateReason
		end
		return false
	end

	local function fn40(arg)
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return arg:GetNetworkPing()
		end)

		ok = ok and type(result) == "number" and result == result
		local n16 = 0.075

		if ok then
			n16 = math.max(result * 2, 0)
		end

		return n16 * 5
	end

	local function fn41(arg)
		return { CFrame = arg, Velocity = nil, SampleTime = os.clock() }
	end

	local function fn42(arg, cFrame, arg2)
		local now = os.clock()
		local n16 = math.max(now - arg.SampleTime, 0.0041666666666666666)
		local n17 = cFrame.Position - arg.CFrame.Position
		local velocity = n17 / n16

		if n17.Magnitude > n6 or velocity.Magnitude > n7 then
			arg.Velocity = Vector3.zero
		elseif arg.Velocity then
			arg.Velocity = arg.Velocity:Lerp(velocity, 1 - math.exp(-n5 * n16))
		else
			arg.Velocity = velocity
		end

		arg.CFrame = cFrame
		arg.SampleTime = now
		local rotation = cFrame.Rotation
		return CFrame.new(cFrame.Position + arg.Velocity * arg2) * rotation
	end

			MaximumLead = fn40(arg2),
			NextLeadUpdate = now + 0.25,
			UseMaximum = false,
			NextEndpointUpdate = now + n4,
		}
	end

			end

				return
			end

					return

				v37[arg] = arg2

			end)
		end

		end

				if v37:GetState() == fallingDown then
					v37:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
				end
			end

			if tbl17.AntiSit then
				fn58(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, false)

				if v37.Sit then
					fn57("Sit", false, false)
					v37:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
				end
			end
		end

		local function fn72()
			if not tbl17.AnimationBreaker or not humanoid then
				return
			end
			local now = os.clock()
			if now - n22 < 0.028 then
				return
			end
			n22 = now
			local playingAnimationTracks = humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
			local animationBreakerLag = tbl17.AnimationBreakerLag

			if animationBreakerLag > 0 then
				local flag11 = true
				local flag12 = false

				if now - n23 > animationBreakerLag / 150 then
					n23 = now
					flag11 = false
					flag12 = true
				end

				for _, playingAnimationTrack in ipairs(playingAnimationTracks) do
					pcall(function()
						if flag11 then
							playingAnimationTrack.TimePosition = obj3[playingAnimationTrack] or playingAnimationTrack.TimePosition
						else
							obj3[playingAnimationTrack] = playingAnimationTrack.TimePosition + now - n24
						end
					end)
				end

				if flag12 then
					n24 = now
				end
			end

			local n25 = tbl17.AnimationBreakerJitter / 100

			if n25 > 0 then
				for _, playingAnimationTrack in ipairs(playingAnimationTracks) do
					pcall(function()
						local n26 = 0.5 + n25 * 3.75
						playingAnimationTrack.TimePosition = -playingAnimationTrack.TimePosition * (1 + n25 / 4 + math.random() * n26)
					end)
				end
			end

			for _, playingAnimationTrack in ipairs(playingAnimationTracks) do
				pcall(function()
					if playingAnimationTrack.Looped then
						obj4[playingAnimationTrack] = nil
						return
					end
					local v39 = obj4[playingAnimationTrack]

					if not v39 then
						obj4[playingAnimationTrack] = now
					elseif playingAnimationTrack.Length > 0 and now - v39 > playingAnimationTrack.Length then
						playingAnimationTrack:Stop()
						obj4[playingAnimationTrack] = nil
					end
				end)
			end
		end

		local function fn77(arg)
			fn64()

			if not flag4 and not flag5 then
				fn72()
			end

			local v39 = humanoid
			local parent = humanoid

			if v39 then
				parent = v39.Parent
			end

			local v40 = parent and fn18(parent)

			if not v39 or v39.Health <= 0 or not v40 or fn76() then
				restoreNoclip()
				restoreSpinbot()
				restoreSlippery()
				n19 = 0
				n20 = 0
				return
			end

			if tbl17.Noclip then
				fn59(parent)
			else
				restoreNoclip()
			end

			if tbl17.Flight then
				restoreSlippery()
				local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
				local moveDirection = v39.MoveDirection
				local n25 = UserInputService2:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) and 1 or 0

				if n25 == 0 and currentCamera then
					local n26 = math.clamp(moveDirection.Magnitude, 0, 1)

					if UserInputService2:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
						n25 = currentCamera.CFrame.LookVector.Y * n26
					elseif UserInputService2:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
						n25 = -currentCamera.CFrame.LookVector.Y * n26
					end
				end

				v40.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 1.1415926, 0)
				local flightSpeed = tbl17.FlightSpeed
				v40.CFrame = v40.CFrame + Vector3.new(moveDirection.X, n25, moveDirection.Z) * arg * flightSpeed * 50
			elseif tbl17.Speed then
				local vector4 = Vector3.new(v39.MoveDirection.X, 0, v39.MoveDirection.Z)
				local n25 = tbl17.SpeedValue * 16
				local speedStyle = tbl17.SpeedStyle

				if speedStyle == "Slippery" then
					if v35 ~= v40 then
						restoreSlippery()
						v35 = v40
						local assemblyLinearVelocity = v40.AssemblyLinearVelocity
						vector3 = Vector3.new(assemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, assemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
					end

					local flag11 = not tbl17.SlipperyStopInAir or v39.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air

					if vector4.Magnitude > 0 and flag11 then
						local exponential = Enum.EasingStyle.Exponential
						local out = Enum.EasingDirection.Out
						vector3 = vector3:Lerp(vector4 * arg * n25 * 150, 0.35 * TweenService:GetValue(math.clamp(arg, 0, 1), exponential, out)) * (1 + arg)
					elseif not flag11 then
						vector3 *= math.max(0, 1 - arg * 4)
					end

					local vector5 = Vector3.new(vector3.X, v40.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, vector3.Z)
					v40.AssemblyLinearVelocity = vector5
					v36 = vector5
				else
					restoreSlippery()
					v40.CFrame = v40.CFrame + vector4 * arg * n25

					if vector4.Magnitude > 0 and v39.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
						if speedStyle == "Bhop" then
							v39:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
						elseif speedStyle == "Minecraft" and os.clock() >= n20 then
							v39:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
							local assemblyLinearVelocity = v40.AssemblyLinearVelocity
							local jumpPower = v39.UseJumpPower and v39.JumpPower or math.sqrt(math.max(0, 2 * workspace.Gravity * v39.JumpHeight))
							local z = assemblyLinearVelocity.Z
							v40.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(assemblyLinearVelocity.X, math.max(assemblyLinearVelocity.Y * 1.5, jumpPower), z)
							n19 = os.clock() + math.max(jumpPower / 500, 0.05)
							n20 = os.clock() + 0.2
						end
					end

					if speedStyle == "Minecraft" and n19 > 0 and os.clock() >= n19 then
						local assemblyLinearVelocity = v40.AssemblyLinearVelocity
						local z = assemblyLinearVelocity.Z
						v40.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(assemblyLinearVelocity.X, -math.abs(assemblyLinearVelocity.Y) * 1.1, z)
						n19 = 0
					end
				end
			else
				restoreSlippery()
				n19 = 0
				n20 = 0
			end

			if tbl17.Spinbot then
				if v34 ~= v39 then
					restoreSpinbot()
					v34 = v39
					autoRotate = v39.AutoRotate
				end

				v39.AutoRotate = false
				v40.CFrame = v40.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, tbl17.SpinbotSpeed / 3 * arg, 0)
			else
				restoreSpinbot()
			end
		end

		tbl:CreateConnection(Players.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function(arg)
			task.defer(function()
				local humanoid2 = arg:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)

			local function fn93(arg)

			local function fn94(arg, sourceInstance)
				local v40 = mellstroyMicUpVoice2.Analyzers[arg]
				if v40 and v40.Input == sourceInstance and v40.Analyzer and v40.Analyzer.Parent then
					return v40.Analyzer
				end
				fn93(arg)
				local audioAnalyzer = fn92(sourceInstance, "AudioAnalyzer")
				if audioAnalyzer then
					mellstroyMicUpVoice2.Analyzers[arg] = { Analyzer = audioAnalyzer, Input = sourceInstance, Owned = false }
					return audioAnalyzer
				end
				local audioAnalyzer2 = nil
				local wire = nil

				if not pcall(function()
					audioAnalyzer2 = Instance.new("AudioAnalyzer")
					audioAnalyzer2.Name = "MellstroyVoiceAnalyzer_" .. tostring(arg.UserId)
					audioAnalyzer2.Parent = screenGui
					wire = Instance.new("Wire")
					wire.Name = "MellstroyVoiceWire_" .. tostring(arg.UserId)
					wire.SourceInstance = sourceInstance
					wire.TargetInstance = audioAnalyzer2
					wire.Parent = screenGui
				end) or not audioAnalyzer2 or not wire then
					if wire then
						pcall(wire.Destroy, wire)
					end

					if audioAnalyzer2 then
						pcall(audioAnalyzer2.Destroy, audioAnalyzer2)
					end

					return nil
				end

				mellstroyMicUpVoice2.Analyzers[arg] = { Analyzer = audioAnalyzer2, Input = sourceInstance, Owned = true, Wire = wire }
				return audioAnalyzer2
			end

			local function fn95(arg, arg2)

								return descendant

						end
					end
				end

				return character2:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("AudioEmitter", true)
			end

				end)
			end

			local function fn98(arg)

			local function fn99(arg)
				if type(arg) ~= "table" then
					return
				end
				local now = os.clock()

				for k, v40 in pairs(arg) do
					if type(v40) == "table" then
						local v41 = fn97(k, v40)

						if v41 then
							local tbl25 = mellstroyMicUpVoice2.LegacyParticipants[v41] or {
								Muted = false,
								SignalActive = false,
								Subscribed = true,
								LastSignalActive = -math.huge,
							}

							if v40.isMuted ~= nil or v40.isMutedLocally ~= nil then
								tbl25.Muted = v40.isMuted == true or v40.isMutedLocally == true
							end

							if v40.subscriptionCompleted ~= nil then
								tbl25.Subscribed = v40.subscriptionCompleted ~= false
							end

							if v40.isSignalActive ~= nil then
								tbl25.SignalActive = v40.isSignalActive == true

								if tbl25.SignalActive then
									tbl25.LastSignalActive = now
								end
							end

							tbl25.LastUpdate = now
							mellstroyMicUpVoice2.LegacyParticipants[v41] = tbl25
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local function fn100(...)
				local v40 = table.pack(...)

				if v40.n >= 3 then
					fn98(v40[1])
					fn99(v40[2])
					fn99(v40[3])
				else
					for i = 1, v40.n do
						fn99(v40[i])
					end
				end
			end

			mellstroyMicUpVoice2.SetRadius = function(arg, arg2)
				local num = tonumber(arg2)

				if arg2 == math.huge or num and num >= 1000 then
					arg.VoiceRadius = math.huge
				else
					arg.VoiceRadius = math.clamp(num or 500, 80, 999)
				end

				local v40 = fn81(arg.VoiceRadius)

				for k in pairs(arg.EmitterCurves) do
					if k and k.Parent then
						pcall(function()
							k:SetDistanceAttenuation(v40)
						end)
					end
				end

				fn78(arg, arg.VoiceRadius)
				return arg.VoiceRadius
			end

			local function fn101(deltaTime)
				if not mellstroyMicUpVoice2.Running then
					return
				end
				local now = os.clock()

				if mellstroyMicUpVoice2.NextInputRefresh <= now then
					mellstroyMicUpVoice2.NextInputRefresh = now + 0.75
					fn90()
				end

				local tbl25 = {}

				for _, player in ipairs(Players2:GetPlayers()) do
					if player ~= localPlayer then
						local v40 = fn89(player)
						local n25 = v40 and fn94(player, v40)
						local v41 = mellstroyMicUpVoice2.LegacyParticipants[player.UserId]
						local v42 = v40 and fn95(player, v40)

						if v42 then
							fn96(v42)
						end

						if v40 and n25 or v41 then
							local v43 = fn86(player)
							local n26 = n25 and (tonumber(n25.RmsLevel) or 0) or 0
							n25 = n25 and (tonumber(n25.PeakLevel) or 0) or 0
							local n27 = math.max(n26, n25 * 0.35)
							local flag11 = v41 and v41.Subscribed and not v41.Muted

							if flag11 then
								flag11 = v41.SignalActive

								if not flag11 then
									flag11 = now - (v41.LastSignalActive or -math.huge) <= mellstroyMicUpVoice2.SignalHoldTime
								end
							end

							local n28

							if flag11 then
								n28 = math.max(n27, 0.72)
							else
								n28 = n27
							end

							v43.SmoothedLevel = v43.SmoothedLevel + (n28 - v43.SmoothedLevel) * (1 - math.exp(-18 * deltaTime))
							local flag12 = false

							pcall(function()
								flag12 = v40.Muted == true
							end)

							flag11 = flag11 or not flag12 and (n26 >= mellstroyMicUpVoice2.SpeakingThreshold or n25 >= mellstroyMicUpVoice2.PeakThreshold)

							if flag11 then
								v43.LastSpoke = now
								v43.FadeStarted = nil
							end

							local n29

							if flag11 or now - v43.LastSpoke <= mellstroyMicUpVoice2.VoiceEndGrace then
								v43.FadeStarted = nil
								n29 = 0
							else
								v43.FadeStarted = v43.FadeStarted or v43.LastSpoke + mellstroyMicUpVoice2.VoiceEndGrace
								n29 = math.clamp((now - v43.FadeStarted) / mellstroyMicUpVoice2.FadeTime, 0, 1)
							end

							v43.Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, math.floor(math.clamp(v43.SmoothedLevel, 0, 1) * 255), 0)
							fn84(v43, n29)

							if v43.Frame.Visible then
								tbl25[#tbl25 + 1] = v43
							end
						else
							if not v40 then
								fn93(player)
							end

							fn85(player)
						end
					end
				end

				table.sort(tbl25, function(arg, arg2)
					local flag11 = now - arg.LastSpoke <= mellstroyMicUpVoice2.VoiceEndGrace
					if flag11 ~= now - arg2.LastSpoke <= mellstroyMicUpVoice2.VoiceEndGrace then
						return flag11
					end

					if math.abs(arg.SmoothedLevel - arg2.SmoothedLevel) > 0.0001 then
						return arg.SmoothedLevel > arg2.SmoothedLevel
					end
					return arg.LastSpoke > arg2.LastSpoke
				end)

				local n25 = 0

				for _, v40 in ipairs(tbl25) do
					if now - v40.LastSpoke <= mellstroyMicUpVoice2.VoiceEndGrace then
						n25 += 1
					end
				end

				local n26 = math.max(mellstroyMicUpVoice2.MaxVisible, n25)

				for i, v40 in ipairs(tbl25) do
					v40.Frame.LayoutOrder = i

					if n26 < i then
						v40.Frame.Visible = false
					end
				end

				if mellstroyMicUpVoice2.LegacyRangeApplied and #mellstroyMicUpVoice2.EmitterCurves == 0 then
					local player = nil

					for _, v40 in ipairs(tbl25) do
						if now - v40.LastSpoke <= mellstroyMicUpVoice2.VoiceEndGrace then
							player = v40.Player
							break
						else
							player = nil
						end
					end

					if player then
						fn83(player)
					else
						fn82()
					end
				else
					fn82()
				end
			end

			mellstroyMicUpVoice2.Stop = function(arg)
				if not arg.Running then
					return
				end
				arg.Running = false

				for _, connection in ipairs(arg.Connections) do
					pcall(function()
						connection:Disconnect()
					end)
				end

				table.clear(arg.Connections)

				for k, emitterCurve in pairs(arg.EmitterCurves) do
					if k and k.Parent then
						pcall(function()
							k:SetDistanceAttenuation(emitterCurve)
						end)
					end
				end

				table.clear(arg.EmitterCurves)
				fn79(arg)
				fn82()

				for k in pairs(arg.Entries) do
					fn85(k)
				end

				for k in pairs(arg.Analyzers) do
					fn93(k)
				end

				table.clear(arg.Inputs)
				table.clear(arg.LegacyParticipants)

				if arg.ScreenGui then
					arg.ScreenGui:Destroy()
				end

				if genv.__MellstroyMicUpVoice == arg then
					genv.__MellstroyMicUpVoice = nil
				end
			end

			mellstroyMicUpVoice2.Connections[#mellstroyMicUpVoice2.Connections + 1] = RunService2.RenderStepped:Connect(fn101)
			mellstroyMicUpVoice2.Connections[#mellstroyMicUpVoice2.Connections + 1] = game.DescendantAdded:Connect(fn88)

			if VoiceChatInternal then
				if pcall(function()
					mellstroyMicUpVoice2.Connections[#mellstroyMicUpVoice2.Connections + 1] = VoiceChatInternal.ParticipantsStateChanged:Connect(fn100)
				end) then
					pcall(function()
						fn100(VoiceChatInternal:GetParticipants())
					end)
				end
			end

			mellstroyMicUpVoice2.Connections[#mellstroyMicUpVoice2.Connections + 1] = Players2.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
				mellstroyMicUpVoice2.Inputs[player] = nil
				fn93(player)
				fn85(player)
			end)

			fn90()
			mellstroyMicUpVoice2:SetRadius(huge)
			return mellstroyMicUpVoice2
		end

		local function fn81()
			local v40 = setclipboard or toclipboard
			if type(v40) ~= "function" then
				warn("[mellstroyhook] clipboard function is unavailable")

			v40(string.format("game:GetService(\"TeleportService\"):TeleportToPlaceInstance(%d, \"%s\")", game.PlaceId, game.JobId))

		local flag12 = false

		local function fn82()
			if flag12 then
				return
			end
			flag12 = true

			task.spawn(function()
				local nextPageCursor = nil
				local huge2 = math.huge
				local v40 = nil
				local huge3 = math.huge
				local v41 = nil

				for i = 1, 3 do
					local str = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. tostring(game.PlaceId) .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"

					if nextPageCursor and nextPageCursor ~= "" then
						str ..= "&cursor=" .. HttpService:UrlEncode(nextPageCursor)
					end

					local ok, result = pcall(function()
						return HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet(str))
					end)

					if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" then
						break
					else
						local v42 = ipairs
						local data = result.data or {}

						for _, v43 in v42(data) do
							local str2 = tostring(v43.id or "")
							local huge4 = tonumber(v43.playing) or math.huge
							local n25 = tonumber(v43.maxPlayers) or 0
							local huge5 = tonumber(v43.ping) or math.huge

							if str2 ~= "" and str2 ~= game.JobId and huge4 > 0 and huge4 < n25 then
								if huge4 > n25 / 2 and huge5 < huge2 then
									huge2 = huge5
									v40 = str2
								end

								if huge5 < huge3 then
									huge3 = huge5
									v41 = str2
								end
							end
						end

						nextPageCursor = result.nextPageCursor

						if not nextPageCursor or nextPageCursor == "" then
							break
						else
						end
					end
				end

				v40 = v40 or v41
				if flag8 then
					flag12 = false
					return
				end

				if v40 then
					local ok, result = pcall(TeleportService.TeleportToPlaceInstance, TeleportService, game.PlaceId, v40, Players.LocalPlayer)
					flag12 = false

					if not ok then
						warn("[mellstroyhook] server hop failed: " .. tostring(result))
					end
				else
					flag12 = false
					warn("[mellstroyhook] no non-full server was found")
				end
			end)
		end

		local function fn52(arg)

				return "Enemy"

		end

		local function fn55(arg)
			if not arg then

			end
			local name = arg.Name
			if tbl2.WeaponCasing == "Uppercase" then
				return string.upper(name)
			end

			if tbl2.WeaponCasing == "Lowercase" then
				return string.lower(name)
			end
			return name
		end

		local font = Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.Code)

		local function fn56(arg)
			local v33 = getcustomasset or getsynasset
			if type(isfile) ~= "function" or type(writefile) ~= "function" or type(v33) ~= "function" then
				return font
			end
			local str = index.Folder .. "/fonts"
			local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
			local tbl19 = arg and { arg } or { "TahomaXP", "ProggyTiny" }

			for _, v34 in ipairs(tbl19) do
				local str2 = str .. "/" .. v34 .. ".ttf"
				local str3 = str .. "/" .. v34 .. ".font.json"

				if isfile(str2) then
					local ok, result = pcall(v33, str2)

					if ok then
						local json = HttpService:JSONEncode({
							name = v34,
							faces = { { name = "Regular", weight = 400, style = "normal", assetId = result } },
						})

						if pcall(writefile, str3, json) then
							local ok2, result2 = pcall(v33, str3)

							if ok2 then
								local ok3, result3 = pcall(Font.new, result2, Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
								if ok3 then
									return result3
								end
								continue
							end

							continue
						end
					end
				end
			end

			return font
		end

			mellstroyFeedback2.SendTrashTalk = function(arg)
				if arg.Unloaded or not tbl2.TrashTalk then
					return false
				end
				local now = os.clock()
				if now - arg.LastTrashTalkAt < math.clamp(tonumber(tbl2.TrashTalkDelay) or 12, 5, 60) then
					return false
				end
				local n17 = #arg.TrashTalkMessages
				if n17 == 0 then
					return false
				end
				local lastTrashTalkIndex = math.random(1, n17)

				if n17 > 1 and lastTrashTalkIndex == arg.LastTrashTalkIndex then
					lastTrashTalkIndex = lastTrashTalkIndex % n17 + 1
				end

				local v35 = arg.TrashTalkMessages[lastTrashTalkIndex]
				local flag8 = false
				local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")

				pcall(function()
					local textChannels = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels")

					if textChannels then
						textChannels = textChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral") or textChannels:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextChannel")
					end

					if textChannels then
						textChannels:SendAsync(v35)
						flag8 = true
					end
				end)

				if not flag8 then
					pcall(function()
						local defaultChatSystemChatEvents = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
						defaultChatSystemChatEvents = defaultChatSystemChatEvents and defaultChatSystemChatEvents:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest")

						if defaultChatSystemChatEvents and defaultChatSystemChatEvents:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
							defaultChatSystemChatEvents:FireServer(v35, "All")
							flag8 = true
						end
					end)
				end

				if flag8 then
					arg.LastTrashTalkAt = now
					arg.LastTrashTalkIndex = lastTrashTalkIndex
				end

				return flag8
			end

			mellstroyFeedback2.PulseHealthshot = function(arg)

				end
				arg.PulseGeneration = arg.PulseGeneration + 1

				local healthshotColor = tbl2.HealthshotColor
				local n17 = math.clamp(tonumber(tbl2.HealthshotDuration) or 1.5, 0.1, 10)
				arg.HealthshotEffect.Enabled = true
				arg.HealthshotEffect.Brightness = 0.08
				arg.HealthshotEffect.Contrast = 0.12
				arg.HealthshotEffect.Saturation = 0.32
				arg.HealthshotEffect.TintColor = healthshotColor
				arg.HealthshotFlash.BackgroundColor3 = healthshotColor
				arg.HealthshotFlash.BackgroundTransparency = 0.88
				TweenService2:Create(arg.HealthshotEffect, TweenInfo.new(n17, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Brightness = 0, Contrast = 0, Saturation = 0, TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) }):Play()
				TweenService2:Create(arg.HealthshotFlash, TweenInfo.new(n17 * 0.8, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()

				task.delay(n17 + 0.05, function()
					if not arg.Unloaded and pulseGeneration == arg.PulseGeneration then
						arg.HealthshotEffect.Enabled = false
					end
				end)
			end

			mellstroyFeedback2.ConfirmHit = function(arg, arg2)
				if arg.Unloaded or not arg2 or arg2.Confirmed then
					return
				end
				arg2.Confirmed = true
				local v35 = string.lower(arg2.Weapon or "weapon")
				arg:Notify("hit player " .. arg:GetPlayerName(arg2.Player) .. " with " .. v35)
				arg:PlayHitSound()
				arg:PulseHealthshot()
				arg:SendTrashTalk()
			end

			mellstroyFeedback2.Tag = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
				if arg.Unloaded or not arg2 or arg2 == Players.LocalPlayer then
					return
				end
				local character = arg2.Character
				local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
					return
				end
				arg.Marks[humanoid] = { Player = arg2, Weapon = arg3, Deadline = os.clock() + (arg3 == "Knife" and 3 or 1.4) }
				if arg.Watched[humanoid] then
					return
				end

				arg.Watched[humanoid] = humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
					local v35 = arg.Marks[humanoid]
					arg.Marks[humanoid] = nil
					arg.Watched[humanoid] = nil
					local flag8

					if v35 then
						local deadline = v35.Deadline
						flag8 = os.clock() <= deadline
					else
						flag8 = v35
					end

					if flag8 then
						arg:ConfirmHit(v35)
					end
				end)
			end

			mellstroyFeedback2.Fling = function(arg, arg2)
				arg:Notify("flinged player " .. arg:GetPlayerName(arg2))
				arg:PlayHitSound()
				arg:PulseHealthshot()
				arg:SendTrashTalk()
			end

			mellstroyFeedback2.Test = function(arg)
				arg:Notify("visual feedback test")
				arg:PlayHitSound()
				arg:PulseHealthshot()
			end

			mellstroyFeedback2.Destroy = function(arg)
				if arg.Unloaded then
					return
				end
				arg.Unloaded = true
				arg.PulseGeneration = arg.PulseGeneration + 1

				for _, v35 in pairs(arg.Watched) do
					pcall(function()
						v35:Disconnect()
					end)
				end

				for _, v35 in ipairs({ arg.Sound, arg.HealthshotEffect, arg.HealthshotFlash, arg.NotificationHolder }) do
					if v35 then
						pcall(v35.Destroy, v35)
					end
				end

				if getgenv().__MellstroyFeedback == arg then
					getgenv().__MellstroyFeedback = nil
				end
			end

			getgenv().__MellstroyFeedback = mellstroyFeedback2

			task.defer(function()
				if not mellstroyFeedback2.Unloaded then
					mellstroyFeedback2:NotifyDiscordInvite()
				end
			end)

		local function fn61(arg)
			local v34 = tbl20[arg]
			if not v34 then
				return
			end

			for _, materialConnection in ipairs(v34.MaterialConnections) do
				materialConnection:Disconnect()
			end

			table.clear(v34.MaterialConnections)

			for k, material in pairs(v34.Materials) do
				if k.Parent then
					k.Material = material.Material
					k.Color = material.Color
					k.Transparency = material.Transparency
					k.Reflectance = material.Reflectance

					if k:IsA("MeshPart") and material.TextureID ~= nil then
						k.TextureID = material.TextureID
					end
				end
			end

			if v34.Humanoid and v34.Humanoid.Parent and v34.DisplayDistanceType then
				v34.Humanoid.DisplayDistanceType = v34.DisplayDistanceType
			end

			for k, v35 in pairs(v34) do
				if typeof(v35) == "Instance" and k ~= "Player" and k ~= "Humanoid" then
					v35:Destroy()
				elseif k == "Skeleton" then
					for _, v36 in ipairs(v35) do
						v36:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end

			tbl20[arg] = nil
		end
