			Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0),
			Visible = false,
			ZIndex = 70,
		})

		local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")

		local tbl23 = {
			Ambient = Lighting.Ambient,
			OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
			Brightness = Lighting.Brightness,
			ClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime,
			FogStart = Lighting.FogStart,
			FogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd,
			GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
		}

		local tbl24 = {}

		local function fn62(arg)
			if not arg then
				return
			end

			for k, v44 in pairs(arg) do
				Lighting[k] = v44
			end

			table.clear(arg)
		end

		tbl2.AmbientColor = tbl23.Ambient
		tbl2.OutdoorAmbientColor = tbl23.OutdoorAmbient

		local tbl25 = {
			Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera,
			FOV = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView or 70,
			FOVApplied = false,
			ZoomApplied = false,
		}

		local tbl26 = {}
		local ColorCorrectionEffect = tbl:RenderObject("ColorCorrectionEffect", { Name = "MellstroyColorCorrection", Enabled = true, Parent = Lighting })
		local BloomEffect = tbl:RenderObject("BloomEffect", { Name = "MellstroyBloom", Enabled = true, Intensity = 0, Parent = Lighting, Size = 24, Threshold = 1 })
		local BlurEffect = tbl:RenderObject("BlurEffect", { Name = "MellstroyBlur", Enabled = true, Parent = Lighting, Size = 0 })

		local Atmosphere = tbl:RenderObject("Atmosphere", {
			Name = "MellstroyAtmosphere",
			Color = tbl2.AtmosphereColor,
			Decay = tbl2.AtmosphereDecay,
			Density = tbl2.AtmosphereDensity,
			Glare = tbl2.AtmosphereGlare,
			Haze = tbl2.AtmosphereHaze,
			Offset = tbl2.AtmosphereOffset,
		})

		local tbl27 = {}

		local function fn63()
			local fullBright = tbl2.FullBright or tbl2.NightMode

			local tbl28 = {
				Ambient = fullBright or tbl2.Ambient,
				OutdoorAmbient = fullBright or tbl2.Ambient,
				Brightness = fullBright,
				GlobalShadows = fullBright,
				ClockTime = fullBright or tbl2.WorldTime,
				FogStart = tbl2.RemoveFog,
				FogEnd = tbl2.RemoveFog,
			}

			for k, v44 in pairs(tbl28) do
				local v45 = tbl24[k]

				if v44 and v45 == nil then
					tbl24[k] = Lighting[k]
				elseif not v44 and v45 ~= nil then
					Lighting[k] = v45
					tbl24[k] = nil
				end
			end

			if tbl2.FullBright then
				Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.Ambient
				Lighting.Brightness = 3
				Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
				Lighting.ClockTime = 14
			elseif tbl2.NightMode then
				Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 60)
				Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.Ambient
				Lighting.Brightness = 1
				Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
				Lighting.ClockTime = 0
			else
				if tbl2.Ambient then
					Lighting.Ambient = tbl2.AmbientColor
					Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = tbl2.OutdoorAmbientColor
				end

				if tbl2.WorldTime then
					Lighting.ClockTime = tbl2.WorldTimeValue
				end
			end

			if tbl2.RemoveFog then
				Lighting.FogStart = 1000000
				Lighting.FogEnd = 1000000
			end

			ColorCorrectionEffect.Saturation = tbl2.Saturation / 100
			ColorCorrectionEffect.Contrast = tbl2.Contrast / 100
			ColorCorrectionEffect.TintColor = tbl2.Tint and tbl2.TintColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			BloomEffect.Intensity = tbl2.Bloom / 10
			BlurEffect.Size = tbl2.Blur

			if tbl2.Atmosphere then
				Atmosphere.Color = tbl2.AtmosphereColor
				Atmosphere.Decay = tbl2.AtmosphereDecay
				Atmosphere.Density = tbl2.AtmosphereDensity
				Atmosphere.Glare = tbl2.AtmosphereGlare
				Atmosphere.Haze = tbl2.AtmosphereHaze
				Atmosphere.Offset = tbl2.AtmosphereOffset
				Atmosphere.Parent = Lighting
			else
				Atmosphere.Parent = nil
			end

			if tbl2.DisablePostProcessing then
				for _, child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("PostEffect") and child ~= ColorCorrectionEffect and child ~= BloomEffect and child ~= BlurEffect then
						if tbl26[child] == nil then
							tbl26[child] = child.Enabled
						end

						child.Enabled = false
					end
				end
			else
				for k, v44 in pairs(tbl26) do
					if k.Parent then
						k.Enabled = v44
					end

					tbl26[k] = nil
				end
			end

			if tbl2.RemoveSkybox then
				for _, child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Sky") then
						tbl27[child] = Lighting
						child.Parent = nil
					end
				end
			else
				for k, v44 in pairs(tbl27) do
					k.Parent = v44
					tbl27[k] = nil
				end
			end
		end

		local v44 = nil

		local function fn64()
			local v45 = v44
			local camera = v44

			if v45 then
				camera = v45.Camera
			end

			if camera and v45.Camera.Parent then
				v45.Camera.CameraType = v45.Type
				v45.Camera.CFrame = v45.CFrame
			end

			v44 = nil
		end

		local function fn65(arg)
			for _, materialConnection in ipairs(arg.MaterialConnections) do
				materialConnection:Disconnect()
			end

			table.clear(arg.MaterialConnections)

			for k, material in pairs(arg.Materials) do
				if k.Parent then
					k.Material = material.Material
					k.Color = material.Color
					k.Transparency = material.Transparency
					k.Reflectance = material.Reflectance

					if k:IsA("MeshPart") and material.TextureID ~= nil then
						k.TextureID = material.TextureID
					end
				end

				arg.Materials[k] = nil
			end

			arg.MaterialSignature = nil
			arg.MaterialCharacter = nil
			arg.MaterialDirty = true
		end

		local function fn66(arg, materialCharacter, color)
			if not tbl2.Material then
				fn65(arg)
				return
			end
			local forceField = tbl2.MaterialName == "ForceField" and Enum.Material.ForceField or Enum.Material.Neon
			local concat = table.concat
			local tbl28 = {}
			local materialName = tbl2.MaterialName
			local str = tostring(color)
			local v45 = tostring
			local materialTransparency = tbl2.MaterialTransparency
			tbl28[1] = materialName
			tbl28[2] = str

			do
				local values = table.pack(v45(materialTransparency))
				table.move(values, 1, values.n, 3, tbl28)
			end

			local v46 = concat(tbl28, "|")
			if arg.MaterialCharacter == materialCharacter and arg.MaterialSignature == v46 and not arg.MaterialDirty then
				return
			end
			fn65(arg)
			arg.MaterialCharacter = materialCharacter
			arg.MaterialSignature = v46

			arg.MaterialConnections[1] = materialCharacter.DescendantAdded:Connect(function()
				arg.MaterialDirty = true
			end)

			arg.MaterialConnections[2] = materialCharacter.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function()
				arg.MaterialDirty = true
			end)

			for _, descendant in ipairs(materialCharacter:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
					arg.Materials[descendant] = {
						Material = descendant.Material,
						Color = descendant.Color,
						Transparency = descendant.Transparency,
						Reflectance = descendant.Reflectance,
						TextureID = descendant:IsA("MeshPart") and descendant.TextureID or nil,
					}

					descendant.Material = descendant.Name == "Head" and Enum.Material.ForceField or forceField
					descendant.Color = color

					if descendant.Transparency < 1 then
						descendant.Transparency = tbl2.MaterialTransparency
					end

					if descendant:IsA("MeshPart") then
						descendant.TextureID = ""
					end
				end
			end

			arg.MaterialDirty = false
		end

		local function fn67(arg, arg2, arg3)
			arg.Box.Visible = false
			arg.BoxStroke.Enabled = false
			arg.BoxOutline.Enabled = false
			arg.BoxInnerOutline.Enabled = false
			arg.GlowStroke.Enabled = false
			arg.HealthOutline.Visible = false
			arg.ArmorOutline.Visible = false
			arg.Name.Visible = false
			arg.HealthText.Visible = false
			arg.Distance.Visible = false
			arg.Weapon.Visible = false
			arg.ToolIcon.Visible = false
			arg.Flags.Visible = false
			arg.Arrow.Visible = false
			arg.Tracer.Visible = false
			arg.HeadDot.Visible = false

			if not arg3 then
				arg.Highlight.Enabled = false
			end

			arg.RadarDot.Visible = false

			for _, v45 in ipairs(arg.Skeleton) do
				v45.Visible = false
			end

			if arg2 then
				fn65(arg)
			end
		end

		local function fn68(arg)
			local attribute = arg:GetAttribute("Armor")
			if type(attribute) == "number" then
				return math.clamp(attribute / 100, 0, 1)
			end
			local bodyEffects = arg:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
			bodyEffects = bodyEffects and bodyEffects:FindFirstChild("Armor")
			if bodyEffects and bodyEffects:IsA("NumberValue") then
				return math.clamp(bodyEffects.Value / 100, 0, 1)
			end
			return 0
		end

		local tbl28 = {
			Vector3.new(-1, -1, -1),
			Vector3.new(-1, 1, -1),
			Vector3.new(-1, 1, 1),
			Vector3.new(-1, -1, 1),
			Vector3.new(1, -1, -1),
			Vector3.new(1, 1, -1),
			Vector3.one,
			Vector3.new(1, -1, 1),
		}

		local tbl29 = {
			Head = true,
			Torso = true,
			["Left Arm"] = true,
			["Right Arm"] = true,
			["Left Leg"] = true,
			["Right Leg"] = true,
			UpperTorso = true,
			LowerTorso = true,
			LeftHand = true,
			LeftLowerArm = true,
			LeftUpperArm = true,
			RightHand = true,
			RightLowerArm = true,
			RightUpperArm = true,
			LeftFoot = true,
			LeftLowerLeg = true,
			LeftUpperLeg = true,
			RightFoot = true,
			RightLowerLeg = true,
			RightUpperLeg = true,
		}

		local function fn69(arg)
			local vector3 = nil
			local vector4 = nil

			for _, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("BasePart") and tbl29[child.Name] then
					local cFrame = child.CFrame
					local size = child.Size
					local position = (cFrame - size * 0.5).Position
					local position2 = (cFrame + size * 0.5).Position

					if vector3 then
						local min = math.min
						local z = vector3.Z
						local z2 = position.Z
						vector3 = Vector3.new(math.min(vector3.X, position.X), math.min(vector3.Y, position.Y), min(z, z2))
						local max = math.max
						local z3 = vector4.Z
						local z4 = position2.Z
						vector4 = Vector3.new(math.max(vector4.X, position2.X), math.max(vector4.Y, position2.Y), max(z3, z4))
					else
						vector3 = position
						vector4 = position2
					end
				end
			end

			if not vector3 then
				return nil
			end
			local n17 = (vector3 + vector4) * 0.5
			local vector5 = Vector3.new(n17.X, n17.Y, vector4.Z)
			return CFrame.new(n17, vector5), vector4 - vector3
		end

		local function fn70(arg, arg2, arg3)
			local n17 = arg3 * 0.5
			local huge = math.huge
			local huge2 = math.huge
			local n18 = -math.huge
			local n19 = -math.huge

			for i = 1, #tbl28 do
				local v45 = arg:WorldToViewportPoint((arg2 + n17 * tbl28[i]).Position)
				huge = math.min(huge, v45.X)
				huge2 = math.min(huge2, v45.Y)
				n18 = math.max(n18, v45.X)
				n19 = math.max(n19, v45.Y)
			end

			local viewportSize = arg.ViewportSize
			local n20 = math.min(huge, viewportSize.X)
			local n21 = math.min(huge2, viewportSize.Y)
			return n20, n21, math.max(n18, 0) - n20, math.max(n19, 0) - n21
		end

		local function fn71(arg)
			for _, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Tool") then
					return child
				end
			end

			return nil
		end

		local function fn72(arg, arg2)
			local player = arg.Player
			local character = player.Character
			local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local v45 = character and fn18(character)

			if humanoid ~= arg.Humanoid then
				if arg.Humanoid and arg.Humanoid.Parent and arg.DisplayDistanceType then
					arg.Humanoid.DisplayDistanceType = arg.DisplayDistanceType
				end

				arg.Humanoid = humanoid
				arg.DisplayDistanceType = humanoid and humanoid.DisplayDistanceType or nil
			end

			if humanoid then
				humanoid.DisplayDistanceType = tbl2.HideNametags and Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None or arg.DisplayDistanceType
			end

			local v46 = fn12(player)

			if not (tbl2.Enabled and not v46.DisableVisuals and character and humanoid and v45 and humanoid.Health > 0) then
				arg.Box.Visible = false
				arg.BoxStroke.Enabled = false
				arg.BoxOutline.Enabled = false
				arg.BoxInnerOutline.Enabled = false
				arg.HealthOutline.Visible = false
				arg.ArmorOutline.Visible = false
				arg.Name.Visible = false
				arg.HealthText.Visible = false
				arg.Distance.Visible = false
				arg.Weapon.Visible = false
				arg.ToolIcon.Visible = false
				arg.Flags.Visible = false
				arg.Arrow.Visible = false
				arg.Tracer.Visible = false
				arg.HeadDot.Visible = false
				arg.Highlight.Enabled = false
				arg.RadarDot.Visible = false

				for _, v47 in ipairs(arg.Skeleton) do
					v47.Visible = false
				end

				fn65(arg)
				return
			end

			local magnitude = (arg2.CFrame.Position - v45.Position).Magnitude

			if tbl2.MaxDistance < magnitude then
				arg.Box.Visible = false
				arg.Highlight.Enabled = false
				arg.Arrow.Visible = false
				arg.RadarDot.Visible = false

				for _, v47 in pairs(arg) do
					if typeof(v47) == "Instance" and v47:IsA("GuiObject") then
						v47.Visible = false
					end
				end

				for _, v47 in ipairs(arg.Skeleton) do
					v47.Visible = false
				end

				fn65(arg)
				return
			end

			local enemyColor = fn9(player) or tbl2.EnemyColor
			local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head") or v45
			local v47, v48 = arg2:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.8, 0))
			local v49, flag8 = arg2:WorldToViewportPoint(v45.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0))
			flag8 = v48 and flag8 and v47.Z > 0 and v49.Z > 0
			arg.Highlight.Adornee = character
			arg.Highlight.Enabled = tbl2.Highlight or tbl2.Chams
			arg.Highlight.FillColor = tbl2.ChamsColor
			arg.Highlight.FillTransparency = tbl2.Chams and 0.35 or 0.7
			arg.Highlight.OutlineColor = enemyColor
			arg.Highlight.OutlineTransparency = tbl2.Highlight and 0 or 1
			arg.Highlight.DepthMode = tbl2.BehindWalls and Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop or Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded

			if tbl2.Material then
				local forceField = tbl2.MaterialName == "ForceField" and Enum.Material.ForceField or Enum.Material.Neon

				for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
						if not arg.Materials[descendant] then
							arg.Materials[descendant] = { Material = descendant.Material, Color = descendant.Color }
						end

						descendant.Material = forceField
						descendant.Color = tbl2.ChamsColor
					end
				end
			else
				fn65(arg)
			end

			if not flag8 then
				arg.Box.Visible = false
				arg.HealthOutline.Visible = false
				arg.ArmorOutline.Visible = false
				arg.Name.Visible = false
				arg.HealthText.Visible = false
				arg.Distance.Visible = false
				arg.Weapon.Visible = false
				arg.ToolIcon.Visible = false
				arg.Flags.Visible = false
				arg.Tracer.Visible = false
				arg.HeadDot.Visible = false

				for _, v50 in ipairs(arg.Skeleton) do
					v50.Visible = false
				end

				if tbl2.Offscreen then
					local v50 = arg2:WorldToViewportPoint(v45.Position)
					local n17 = arg2.ViewportSize * 0.5
					local n18 = Vector2.new(v50.X, v50.Y) - n17

					if v50.Z < 0 then
						n18 = -n18
					end

					if n18.Magnitude < 0.001 then
						n18 = Vector2.new(0, -1)
					end

					local n19 = tbl2.ArrowRange / 100
					local n20 = n17 + n18.Unit * math.min(arg2.ViewportSize.X, arg2.ViewportSize.Y) * n19
					arg.Arrow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
					arg.Arrow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(n20.X, n20.Y)
					arg.Arrow.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(n18.Y, n18.X)) + 90
					arg.Arrow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(tbl2.ArrowSize * 2, tbl2.ArrowSize * 2)
					arg.Arrow.Text = "▲"
					arg.Arrow.TextColor3 = enemyColor
					arg.Arrow.TextSize = tbl2.ArrowSize
					arg.Arrow.Visible = true
				else
					arg.Arrow.Visible = false
				end
			else
				arg.Arrow.Visible = false
				local n17 = math.max(math.abs(v49.Y - v47.Y), 20)
				local n18 = n17 * 0.55
				local n19 = v47.X - n18 * 0.5
				local n20 = math.min(v47.Y, v49.Y)
				arg.Box.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(n19, n20)
				arg.Box.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(n18, n17)
				arg.Box.Visible = tbl2.Box or tbl2.ImageFill
				arg.Fill.BackgroundColor3 = enemyColor
				arg.Fill.BackgroundTransparency = tbl2.ImageFill and tbl2.ImageTransparency or 1
				arg.Fill.Visible = tbl2.Box or tbl2.ImageFill
				arg.GlowStroke.Enabled = false
				arg.BoxStroke.Color = tbl2.BoxColor
				arg.BoxStroke.Enabled = tbl2.Box
				arg.BoxOutline.Color = tbl2.BoxOutlineColor
				arg.BoxOutline.Enabled = tbl2.Box and tbl2.BoxOutline
				arg.BoxInnerOutline.Color = tbl2.BoxOutlineColor
				arg.BoxInnerOutline.Enabled = tbl2.Box and tbl2.BoxOutline
				local n21 = math.clamp(humanoid.Health / humanoid.MaxHealth, 0, 1)
				arg.HealthOutline.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(n19 - 6, n20)
				arg.HealthOutline.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, n17)
				arg.HealthOutline.Visible = tbl2.HealthBar
				arg.Health.BackgroundColor3 = tbl2.HealthColor
				arg.Health.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(1, n17 - 1)
				arg.Health.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2, math.max(n17 - 2, 0) * n21)
				local v50 = fn68(character)
				arg.ArmorOutline.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(n19 + n18 + 2, n20)
				arg.ArmorOutline.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, n17)
				arg.ArmorOutline.Visible = tbl2.ArmorBar and v50 > 0
				arg.Armor.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(1, n17 - 1)
				arg.Armor.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2, math.max(n17 - 2, 0) * v50)
				arg.Name.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(v47.X - 80, n20 - 15)
				arg.Name.Text = tbl2.DisplayName and player.DisplayName or player.Name
				arg.Name.TextColor3 = tbl2.TextColor
				arg.Name.Visible = tbl2.Name
				arg.HealthText.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(n19 - 88, n20 + n17 * (1 - n21) - 7)
				arg.HealthText.Text = tostring(math.floor(humanoid.Health))
				arg.HealthText.TextColor3 = tbl2.HealthColor
				arg.HealthText.Visible = tbl2.HealthText
				arg.Distance.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(v47.X - 80, n20 + n17 + 1)
				arg.Distance.Text = tostring(math.floor(magnitude)) .. "m"
				arg.Distance.TextColor3 = tbl2.TextColor
				arg.Distance.Visible = tbl2.Distance
				local knife = fn71(character)
				arg.Weapon.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(v47.X - 80, n20 + n17 + (tbl2.Distance and 14 or 1))
				arg.Weapon.Text = knife and knife.Name or "-"
				arg.Weapon.TextColor3 = tbl2.TextColor
				arg.Weapon.Visible = tbl2.WeaponText
				arg.ToolIcon.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(v47.X - 20, n20 + n17 + 1)
				local toolIcon = arg.ToolIcon

				if knife then
					knife = knife.Name == "Knife" and tbl11.Knife or knife.Name == "Gun" and tbl11.Revolver or ""
				end

				toolIcon.Image = knife or ""
				arg.ToolIcon.ImageColor3 = tbl2.TextColor
				arg.ToolIcon.Visible = tbl2.WeaponIcon and arg.ToolIcon.Image ~= ""
				arg.Flags.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(n19 + n18 + 7, n20)
				arg.Flags.Text = tbl8[player.Name] or ""
				arg.Flags.TextColor3 = enemyColor
				arg.Flags.TextXAlignment = "Left"
				arg.Flags.Visible = tbl2.Flags and arg.Flags.Text ~= ""
				local v51, v52 = arg2:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position)
				arg.HeadDot.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(v51.X, v51.Y)
				arg.HeadDot.BackgroundColor3 = enemyColor
				arg.HeadDot.Visible = tbl2.HeadDot and v52
				local tracer = tbl2.Tracer
				fn59(arg.Tracer, Vector2.new(arg2.ViewportSize.X * 0.5, arg2.ViewportSize.Y), Vector2.new(v49.X, v49.Y), 1, enemyColor, tracer)

				for i, v53 in ipairs(tbl19) do
					local v54 = character:FindFirstChild(v53[1])
					local v55 = character:FindFirstChild(v53[2])
					local v56 = arg.Skeleton[i]

					if tbl2.Skeleton and v54 and v55 then
						local v57, v58 = arg2:WorldToViewportPoint(v54.Position)
						local v59, v60 = arg2:WorldToViewportPoint(v55.Position)
						fn59(v56, Vector2.new(v57.X, v57.Y), Vector2.new(v59.X, v59.Y), 1, enemyColor, v58 and v60)
					else
						v56.Visible = false
					end
				end
			end

			local character2 = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
			character2 = character2 and fn18(character2)

			if tbl2.Radar and character2 then
				local v50 = character2.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(v45.Position)
				local n17 = 60 / math.max(tbl2.RadarRange, 1)
				local n18 = Vector2.new(v50.X, v50.Z) * n17

				if n18.Magnitude > 59 then
					n18 = n18.Unit * 59
				end

				arg.RadarDot.Parent = v34
				arg.RadarDot.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(65 + n18.X, 65 + n18.Y)
				arg.RadarDot.BackgroundColor3 = enemyColor
				arg.RadarDot.Visible = true
			else
				arg.RadarDot.Visible = false
			end
		end

		local tbl30 = {}

		local function fn73(arg, arg2)
			local instance = Instance.new(arg)
			local v45 = pairs
			arg2 = arg2 or {}

			for k, v46 in v45(arg2) do
				instance[k] = v46
			end

			return instance
		end

		local tbl31 = {}

		local function fn74(arg, arg2)
			if typeof(arg) ~= "Vector3" or typeof(arg2) ~= "Vector3" then
				return
			end
			local magnitude = (arg2 - arg).Magnitude
			if magnitude ~= magnitude or magnitude < 0.01 then
				return
			end
			local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
			if not currentCamera then
				return
			end
			local n17 = math.max(tonumber(tbl2.BulletTracerDuration) or 1.2, 0.05)
			local n18 = math.clamp(tonumber(tbl2.BulletTracerFadeAfter) or 0, 0, n17)
			local n19 = math.clamp(tonumber(tbl2.BulletTracerWidth) or 0.08, 0.01, 2)

			local Part = fn73("Part", {
				Name = "MellstroyBulletTracer",
				Anchored = true,
				CanCollide = false,
				CanQuery = false,
				CanTouch = false,
				CastShadow = false,
				Color = tbl2.BulletTracerColor,
				Material = tbl2.BulletTracerMaterial or Enum.Material.Neon,
				Size = Vector3.new(n19, n19, magnitude),
				CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(arg, arg2) * CFrame.new(0, 0, -magnitude / 2),
				Transparency = 0,
				Parent = currentCamera,
			})

			tbl31[Part] = true

			if tbl2.BulletTracerFade then
				local n20 = n17 - n18

				task.delay(n18, function()
					if not Part.Parent then
						return
					end

					if n20 <= 0 then
						Part.Transparency = 1
						return
					end

					pcall(function()
						TweenService:Create(Part, TweenInfo.new(n20, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
					end)
				end)
			end

			task.delay(n17, function()
				tbl31[Part] = nil

				if Part.Parent then
					Part:Destroy()
				end
			end)

			return Part
		end

		local function fn75(arg)
			local tbl32 = arg or {}

			if tbl32.BorderSizePixel == nil then
				tbl32.BorderSizePixel = 0
			end

			if tbl32.Parent == nil then
				tbl32.Parent = Frame
			end

			if tbl32.ZIndex == nil then
				tbl32.ZIndex = 50
			end

			return fn73("Frame", tbl32)
		end

		local function fn76(arg)
			return fn73("TextLabel", {
				Name = arg,
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				BorderSizePixel = 0,
				Font = Enum.Font.Code,
				Parent = Frame,
				Size = UDim2.fromOffset(160, 14),
				Text = "",
				TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
				TextSize = 13,
				TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
				TextStrokeTransparency = 0,
				TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
				Visible = false,
				ZIndex = 54,
			})
		end

		local function fn77(arg)
			for _, object in ipairs(arg.Objects) do
				if typeof(object) == "Instance" and object.Parent then
					object:Destroy()
				end
			end
		end

		local function fn78(arg)
			if #arg.Objects > 0 then
				tbl30[#tbl30 + 1] = arg
			end
		end

		local tbl32 = {}

		local function fn79(arg, arg2)
			if tbl32[arg] ~= arg2 then
				return
			end
			tbl32[arg] = nil

			if arg2.HealthConnection then
				arg2.HealthConnection:Disconnect()
			end

			if arg2.DiedConnection then
				arg2.DiedConnection:Disconnect()
			end
		end

		local function fn80(arg, textColor3, arg2)
			local tbl33 = {
				Started = os.clock(),
				Lifetime = 0.8,
				Origin = arg.OriginPosition,
				Aim = arg.AimPosition,
				Color = textColor3,
				Objects = {},
				Skeleton = {},
			}

			if tbl2.HitMarker then
				tbl33.Marker = {}

				for i = 1, 4 do
					local v45 = fn75({
						Name = "HitMarker" .. i,
						BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
						Visible = false,
						ZIndex = 63,
					})

					tbl33.Marker[i] = v45
					table.insert(tbl33.Objects, v45)
				end
			end

			if tbl2.HitParticle then
				tbl33.Particle = fn76("HitParticle")
				tbl33.Particle.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
				tbl33.Particle.Text = "✦"
				tbl33.Particle.TextColor3 = textColor3
				tbl33.Particle.TextSize = 18
				table.insert(tbl33.Objects, tbl33.Particle)
			end

			if tbl2.HitSkeleton and arg2 then
				for i, v45 in ipairs(tbl19) do
					local v46 = arg2:FindFirstChild(v45[1])
					local v47 = arg2:FindFirstChild(v45[2])

					if v46 and v47 then
						local v48 = fn75({
							Name = "HitSkeletonOutline" .. i,
							BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15),
							Visible = false,
							ZIndex = 62,
						})

						local v49 = fn75({ Name = "HitSkeleton" .. i, BackgroundColor3 = textColor3, Visible = false, ZIndex = 63 })
						tbl33.Skeleton[#tbl33.Skeleton + 1] = { A = v46.Position, B = v47.Position, Outline = v48, Line = v49 }
						table.insert(tbl33.Objects, v48)
						table.insert(tbl33.Objects, v49)
					end
				end
			end

			fn78(tbl33)
		end

		local function fn81(arg, arg2)
			local flag8 = not arg.Target

			if not flag8 then
				flag8 = not (tbl2.HitMarker or tbl2.HitParticle or tbl2.HitSkeleton)
			end

			if flag8 then
				return
			end
			local v45 = Players:FindFirstChild(arg.Target)
			local character = v45 and v45.Character
			local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if not humanoid then
				return
			end
			local targetHealth = arg.TargetHealth or humanoid.Health
			if humanoid.Health < targetHealth then
				fn80(arg, arg2, character)
				return
			end

			if humanoid.Health <= 0 then
				return
			end
			local v46 = tbl32[humanoid]

			if v46 then
				fn79(humanoid, v46)
			end

			local tbl33 = {}
			tbl32[humanoid] = tbl33

			local function fn82()
				if tbl32[humanoid] ~= tbl33 then
					return
				end
				fn79(humanoid, tbl33)
				fn80(arg, arg2, character)
			end

			tbl33.HealthConnection = humanoid.HealthChanged:Connect(function(health)
				if health < targetHealth then
					fn82()
				end
			end)

			tbl33.DiedConnection = humanoid.Died:Connect(fn82)

			task.delay(1.25, function()
				fn79(humanoid, tbl33)
			end)
		end

		local function fn82(arg)
			local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
			if not currentCamera or not arg or not arg.AimPosition then
				return
			end
			local enemyColor = tbl2.EnemyColor

			if arg.Target then
				local v45 = Players:FindFirstChild(arg.Target)

				if v45 then
					enemyColor = fn54(fn52(v45))
				end
			end

			local tbl33 = {
				Started = os.clock(),
				Lifetime = 0.8,
				BaseLifetime = 0.8,
				Origin = arg.OriginPosition or currentCamera.CFrame.Position,
				Aim = arg.AimPosition,
				Color = enemyColor,
				Objects = {},
				Skeleton = {},
			}

			if tbl2.BulletTracers then
				fn74(tbl33.Origin, tbl33.Aim)
			end

			if tbl2.BulletImpacts then
				tbl33.Impact = fn73("Part", {
					Name = "MellstroyShotImpact",
					Anchored = true,
					CanCollide = false,
					CanQuery = false,
					CanTouch = false,
					CastShadow = false,
					Color = enemyColor,
					Material = Enum.Material.Neon,
					Parent = workspace,
					Position = tbl33.Aim,
					Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball,
					Size = Vector3.new(0.35, 0.35, 0.35),
				})

				table.insert(tbl33.Objects, tbl33.Impact)
			end

			fn78(tbl33)
			fn81(arg, enemyColor)
		end

		for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			fn60(player)
		end

		tbl:CreateConnection(Players.PlayerAdded, fn60)
		tbl:CreateConnection(Players.PlayerRemoving, fn61)
		tbl:CreateConnection(RunService.PreRender, function(...) end)

		table.insert(v.CleanupCallbacks, function()
			if flag7 then
				UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = mouseIconEnabled
				flag7 = false
			end

			for _, v45 in pairs(tbl32) do
				if v45.HealthConnection then
					v45.HealthConnection:Disconnect()
				end

				if v45.DiedConnection then
					v45.DiedConnection:Disconnect()
				end
			end

			table.clear(tbl32)

			for i = #tbl30, 1, -1 do
				fn77(tbl30[i])
				tbl30[i] = nil
			end

			for k in pairs(tbl31) do
				tbl31[k] = nil

				if k.Parent then
					k:Destroy()
				end
			end

			for k in pairs(tbl20) do
				fn61(k)
			end

			flag6 = false
			fn64()

			if tbl25.Camera and tbl25.Camera.Parent and tbl25.FOVApplied then
				tbl25.Camera.FieldOfView = tbl25.FOV
			end

			if tbl25.ZoomApplied then
				Players.LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = tbl25.MinZoom
				Players.LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = tbl25.MaxZoom
			end

			if tbl24 then
				fn62(tbl24)
				tbl24 = nil
			end

			for k, v45 in pairs(tbl26) do
				if k.Parent then
					k.Enabled = v45
				end
			end

			for k, v45 in pairs(tbl27) do
				k.Parent = v45
			end

			ColorCorrectionEffect:Destroy()
			BloomEffect:Destroy()
			BlurEffect:Destroy()
			Atmosphere:Destroy()
		end)
